Pucchā – kaṃ āvuso ārabbbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, concerning whom and regarding what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sambahule bhante bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhū akālacīvaraṃ paṭiggahetvā māsaṃ atikkāmesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning several monks. Venerable sir, several monks, having received out-of-season robes, kept them for more than a month. Concerning that matter, venerable sir, it was laid down.

1. Cīvaravagga 4. purāṇacīvara sikkhāpucchā

1. Chapter on Robes 4. Questioning on the Training Rule concerning an Old Robe.

Pucchā – aññātikāya [M 1.0092] āvuso bhikkhuniyā purāṇacīvaraṃ dhovāpentassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the Nissaggiya Pācittiya rule laid down by the Blessed One for having an old robe washed by a bhikkhunī who is not a relative?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī.

Pucchā – kaṃ āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, concerning whom and regarding what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – āyasmantaṃ bhante udāyiṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante udāyī aññātikāya bhikkhuniyā purāṇacīvaraṃ dhovāpesi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the Venerable Udāyī. Venerable sir, the Venerable Udāyī had an old robe washed by a bhikkhunī who was not a relative. Concerning that matter, venerable sir, it was laid down.

1. Cīvaravagga 5. cīvarapaṭiggahaṇa sikkhāpucchā

1. Chapter on Robes 5. Questioning on the Training Rule concerning Receiving a Robe.

Pucchā – aññātikāya [M 1.0093] āvuso bhikkhuniyā hatthato cīvaraṃ paṭiggaṇhantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where did the Blessed One lay down the Nissaggiya Pācittiya rule for accepting a robe from the hand of a bhikkhunī who is not a relative?

Vissajjanā – rājagahe bhante paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Rājagaha.

Pucchā – kaṃ āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, concerning whom and regarding what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – āyasmantaṃ bhante udāyiṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante udāyī aññātikāya bhikkhuniyā hatthato cīvaraṃ paṭiggahesi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the Venerable Udāyī. Venerable sir, the Venerable Udāyī received a robe from the hand of a bhikkhunī who was not a relative. Concerning that matter, venerable sir, it was laid down.

1. Cīvaravagga 6. aññātakaviññatti sikkhāpucchā

1. Chapter on Robes 6. Questioning on the Training Rule concerning Requesting from a Non-relative.

Pucchā – aññātakaṃ [M 1.0095] āvuso gahapatiṃvā gahatāniṃvā cīvaraṃ viññāpentassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the Nissaggiya Pācittiya rule laid down by the Blessed One for one who requests a robe from a householder or householder's wife who is not a relative?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī.

Pucchā – kaṃ āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, concerning whom and regarding what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – āyasmantaṃ bhante upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante upanando sakyaputto aññātakaṃ seṭṭhiputtaṃ cīvaraṃ viññāpesi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the Venerable Upananda the Sakyan. Venerable sir, the Venerable Upananda the Sakyan requested a robe from the son of a merchant who was not a relative. Concerning that matter, venerable sir, it was laid down.

1. Cīvaravagga 7. tatuttari sikkhāpucchā

1. Chapter on Robes 7. Questioning on the Training Rule concerning More than That.

Pucchā – aññātakaṃ [M 1.0096] āvuso gahapatiṃ vā gahapatāniṃ vā tatuttari cīvaraṃ viññāpentassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the Nissaggiya Pācittiya rule laid down by the Blessed One for requesting a robe beyond that from a householder or householder's wife who is not a relative?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī.

Pucchā – kaṃ āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, concerning whom and regarding what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – chabbaggiye bhante bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū na mattaṃ jānitvā bahumpi cīvaraṃ viññāpesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the group-of-six monks. Venerable sir, the group-of-six monks, not knowing moderation, requested many robes. Concerning that matter, venerable sir, it was laid down.

Pucchā – pubbe [M 1.0097] appavāritassa āvuso aññātakaṃ gahapatiṃ vā gahatāniṃ vā upasaṅkamitvā cīvare vikappaṃ āpajjantassa ca aññātake gahapatike upasaṅkamitvā cīvare vikappaṃ āpajjantassa ca nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the Nissaggiya Pācittiya rule laid down by the Blessed One for one who, not having been previously invited, approaches a householder or householder's wife who is not a relative and makes a suggestion about a robe, and for one who approaches householders who are not relatives and makes a suggestion about a robe?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī.

Pucchā – kaṃ āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, concerning whom and regarding what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – āyasmantaṃ bhante upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante upanando sakyaputto pubbe appavārito aññātakaṃ gahapatikaṃ upasaṅkamitvā cīvare vikappaṃ āpajji. Soyeva bhante āyasmā upanando sakyaputto pubbe appavārito aññātake gahapatike upasaṅkamitvā cīvare vikappaṃ āpajji, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the Venerable Upananda the Sakyan. Venerable sir, the Venerable Upananda the Sakyan, not having been previously invited, approached a householder who was not a relative and made a suggestion about a robe. That same Venerable Upananda the Sakyan, venerable sir, not having been previously invited, approached householders who were not relatives and made a suggestion about a robe. Concerning that matter, venerable sir, it was laid down.

1. Cīvaravagga 10. rāja sikkhāpucchā

1. Chapter on Robes 10. Questioning on the Training Rule concerning a King.

Pucchā – atirekatikkhattuṃ āvuso codanāya atireka chakkhattuṃ ṭhānena cīvaraṃ abhinipphādentassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the Nissaggiya Pācittiya rule laid down by the Blessed One for one who has a robe produced by being reminded more than three times and by being pressed more than six times?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī.

Pucchā – kaṃ [M 1.0099] āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, concerning whom and in what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – āyasmantaṃ bhante upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante upanando sakyaputto upāsakena ajjaṇho bhante āgamehīti vuccamāno nāgamesi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the Venerable Upananda the Sakyan. Venerable sir, when the Venerable Upananda the Sakyan was told by a lay follower, 'Venerable sir, please come tomorrow,' he did not come. Venerable sir, it was laid down in that matter.

2. Kosiyavagga, 1. kosiya sikkhāpucchā

2. The Chapter on Silk, 1. Questioning on the Training Rule concerning Silk.

Pucchā – kosiyamissakaṃ āvuso santhakaṃ kārāpentassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the nissaggiya pācittiya rule laid down by the Blessed One regarding one who has a rug made with a mixture of silk?

Vissajjanā – āḷaviyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Āḷavī.

Pucchā – kaṃ [M 1.0100]0 āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, concerning whom and in what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – chabbaggiye bhante ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū kosiyakārake upasaṅkamitvā evamahaṃsu bahū āvuso kosakārake pacatha amhākampi dassatha mayampi icchāma kosiyamissakaṃ santakaṃ kātunti, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the group-of-six monks. Venerable sir, the group-of-six monks approached the silk-rug makers and said: 'Friends, you are making many silk rugs; give some to us also. We too wish to have a rug mixed with silk made.' Venerable sir, it was laid down in that matter.

2. Kosiyavagga 2. suddhakāḷaka sikkhāpucchā

2. The Silk Chapter 2. The Question on the Pure Black Wool Rule

Pucchā – suddhakāḷakānaṃ āvuso eḷakalomānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpentassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the nissaggiya pācittiya rule laid down by the Blessed One concerning one who has a rug made of pure black sheep's wool?

Vissajjanā – vesāliyaṃ bhante paññatthaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Vesālī.

Pucchā – kaṃ [M 1.0101] āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, concerning whom and in what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – chabbaggiye bhante bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū suddhakāḷakānaṃ eḷakalomānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the group-of-six monks. Venerable sir, the group-of-six monks had a rug made of pure black sheep's wool. Venerable sir, it was laid down in that matter.

2. Kosiyavagga 3. dvebhāga sikkhāpucchā

2. The Silk Chapter 3. The Question on the Two-Parts Rule

Pucchā – anādiyitvā āvuso tulaṃ odātānaṃ tulaṃ gocariyānaṃ navaṃ santhataṃ kārāpentassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the nissaggiya pācittiya rule laid down by the Blessed One concerning one who has a new rug made without taking a portion of white wool and a portion of tawny wool?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī.

Pucchā – kaṃ [M 1.0102] āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, concerning whom and in what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – chabbaggiye bhante bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū thokaṃyeva odātaṃ ante ādiyitvā tatheva suddhakāḷakānaṃ eḷakalomānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the group-of-six monks. Venerable sir, the group-of-six monks, taking just a little white wool at the edge, had a rug made of only pure black sheep's wool. Venerable sir, it was laid down in that matter.

2. Kosiyavagga 4. chabbassa sikkhāpucchā

2. The Silk Chapter 4. The Question on the Six Years Rule

Pucchā – yaṃ tena āvuso bhagavatā…pe… sammāsambuddhena anuvassaṃ kārāpentassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the nissaggiya pācittiya rule laid down by that Blessed One… the Perfectly Enlightened One, concerning one who has a rug made annually?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī.

Pucchā – kaṃ [M 1.0103] āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, concerning whom and in what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sambahule bhante bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhū anuvassaṃ santhataṃ kārāpesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning a number of monks. Venerable sir, a number of monks had a rug made annually. Venerable sir, it was laid down in that matter.

Pucchā – kassa āvuso vacanaṃ.

Question: Friend, whose word is this?

Vissajjanā – bhagavato bhante vacanaṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa.

Reply: Venerable sir, it is the word of the Blessed One, the Arahant, the Perfectly Enlightened One.

Pucchā – kenāvuso ābhataṃ.

Question: Friend, by whom was it handed down?

Vissajjanā – paramparāya bhante ābhataṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it has been handed down by tradition.

Pucchā – atthi [M 1.0104] nukho āvuso ettha koci viraddhadoso.

Question: Friend, is there any fault of error herein?

Vissajjanā – natthi bhante.

Reply: Venerable sir, there is none.

Saṃgāyanatthāya saṅghassa ñāpanaṃ

An Announcement to the Saṅgha for the Purpose of Recitation

Suṇātu me bhante saṅgho, ahaṃ āyasmantaṃ vicittasārābhivaṃsaṃ tipiṭakadhara dhammabhaṇḍāgārikaṃ imesaṃ nissaggiyapācittiyasikkhāpadānaṃ yāva chabbassasikkhāpadaṃ nidānādīni pucchitabbāni anekāni ṭhānāni pucchiṃ, puṭṭhopuṭṭho ca so āyasmā vicittasārābhivaṃso vissajjesi, itihidaṃ bhante imāni sikkhāpadāni nimmalāni suparisuddhānī tasseva bhagavato vacanāni arahato sammāsambuddhassa, tasmā yathāpure mahākassapādayo mahātheravarā porāṇasaṃgītikārā brahmacariyassa ciraṭṭhitiyā bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya lokānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya devamanussānaṃ dhammavinayaṃ saṃgāyiṃsu ceva anusaṃgāyiṃsu ca, evameva mayampi dāni sabbeva chaṭṭhasaṃgīti mahādhammasabhā pariyāpannā chabbassa nissaggiya sikkhāpadato paṭṭhāya imāni nissaggiyasikkhāpadāni ekato gaṇasajjhāyaṃ katvā saṃhāyeyyāma.

Venerable sirs, may the Saṅgha hear me. I have questioned the Venerable Vicittasārābhivaṃsa, the bearer of the Tipiṭaka, the keeper of the Dhamma treasury, on many points to be questioned concerning these nissaggiya pācittiya training rules up to the six-years training rule, including their origins. And being questioned again and again, that Venerable Vicittasārābhivaṃsa has answered. Thus, venerable sirs, these training rules are stainless and perfectly pure, being the words of that same Blessed One, the Arahant, the Perfectly Enlightened One. Therefore, just as in the past the great senior elders, such as Mahākassapa and others, the ancient compilers of the recitations, recited and re-recited the Dhamma and Vinaya for the long-lasting stability of the holy life, for the welfare of the many, for the happiness of the many, out of compassion for the world, for the benefit, welfare, and happiness of gods and humans, even so should we now, all who are part of this Sixth Council's great Dhamma assembly, beginning from the six-years nissaggiya training rule, having made a group recitation, chant these nissaggiya training rules together.

2. Kosiyavagga 5. nisīdanasanthata sikkhāpucchā

2. The Silk Chapter 5. The Question on the Sitting-Rug Rule

Pucchā – yaṃ [M 1.0105] tena āvuso bhagavatā…pe… sammāsambuddhena anādiyitvā purāṇasanthatassa sāmantā sugatavidattiṃ navaṃ nisīdanasanthataṃ kārāpentassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the nissaggiya pācittiya rule laid down by that Blessed One… the Perfectly Enlightened One, concerning one who has a new sitting-rug made without including a Sugata-span from the border of the old rug?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī.

Pucchā – kaṃ [M 1.0106] āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The question: With reference to whom, friend, and concerning what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sambahule bhante bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhū santhatāni ujjhitvā āraññikaṃ piṇḍapātikaṅgaṃ paṃsukūlakaṅgaṃ samādiyiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning a number of monks. Venerable sir, a number of monks, having discarded their rugs, undertook the practices of forest-dwelling, alms-seeking, and wearing rag-robes. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that matter.

2. Kosiyavagga 6. eḷakaloma sikkhāpucchā

2. The Kosiyavagga 6: Inquiry concerning the training rule on sheep's wool

Pucchā – eḷakalomāni āvuso paṭiggahetvā tiyojanaṃ atikkāmentassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ.

The question: Friend, where was it laid down that it is an offense entailing relinquishment and confession for one who, having accepted sheep's wool, travels more than three yojanas?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ.

The reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī.

Pucchā – kaṃ [M 1.0107] āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The question: With reference to whom, friend, and concerning what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – aññataraṃ bhante bhikkhuṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, aññataro bhante bhikkhu eḷakalomāni paṭiggahetvā tiyojanaṃ atikkāmesi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning a certain monk. Venerable sir, a certain monk, having accepted sheep's wool, traveled more than three yojanas. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that matter.

2. Kosiyavagga 7. eḷakalomadhovāpana sikkhāpucchā

2. The Kosiyavagga 7: Inquiry concerning the training rule on washing sheep's wool

Pucchā – aññātikāya [M 1.0108] āvuso bhikkhuniyā eḷakalomāni dhovāpentassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ.

The question: Friend, where was it laid down that it is an offense entailing relinquishment and confession for one who has sheep's wool washed by a bhikkhunī who is not a relative?

Vissajjanā – sakkesu bhante paññattaṃ.

The reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down among the Sakyans.

Pucchā – kaṃ āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The question: With reference to whom, friend, and concerning what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – chabbaggiye bhante bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū aññātikāhi bhikkhūnīhi eḷakalomāni dhovāpesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the group-of-six bhikkhus. Venerable sir, the group-of-six bhikkhus had sheep's wool washed by bhikkhunīs who were not relatives. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that matter.

2. Kosiyavagga 8. rūpiya sikkhāpucchā

2. The Kosiyavagga 8: Inquiry concerning the training rule on silver

Pucchā – rūpiyaṃ [M 1.0109] āvuso paṭiggaṇhantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ.

The question: Friend, where was it laid down by the Blessed One that it is an offense entailing relinquishment and confession for one who accepts silver?

Vissajjanā – rājagahe bhante paññattaṃ.

The reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Rājagaha.

Pucchā – kaṃ āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The question: With reference to whom, friend, and concerning what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – āyasmantaṃ bhante upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante upanando sakyaputto rūpiyaṃ paṭiggahesi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the Venerable Upananda the Sakyan. Venerable sir, the Venerable Upananda the Sakyan accepted silver. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that matter.

Pucchā – kathaṃ [M 1.0110] āvuso tattha paṭipajjanto rūpiyaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti nāma.

The question: How, friend, does one behave so as to be called one who accepts silver?

Vissajjanā – sayaṃ bhante gaṇhāti, aññaṃ vā gaṇhāpesi, idaṃ ayyassa hotūti upanikkhittaṃ sādiyati, evaṃ kho bhante paṭipajjanto bhikkhu rūpiyaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti nāma.

The reply: Venerable sir, he himself takes it, or he has another take it, or he approves of it being placed near him, thinking, 'This is for the venerable one.' Venerable sir, a bhikkhu behaving in this way is said to be one who accepts silver.

Pucchā – kathaṃ āvuso tattha paṭipajjanto bhikkhu rūpiyaṃ na paṭiggaṇhāti nāma.

The question: How, friend, does a bhikkhu behave so as not to be called one who accepts silver?

Vissajjanā – yathā [M 1.0111] bhante rājasikkhāpade yathā ca bhante meṇḍakasikkhāpade āgacchati, tathā bhante paṭipajjanto bhikkhu rūpiyaṃ na paṭiggaṇhātināma.

The reply: Venerable sir, just as it comes in the training rule concerning the king, and just as in the training rule concerning Meṇḍaka, so too, venerable sir, a bhikkhu behaving in this way is not said to be one who accepts silver.

2. Kosiyavagga 9. rūpiyasaṃvohāra sikkhāpucchā

2. The Kosiyavagga 9: Inquiry concerning the training rule on monetary transactions

Pucchā – rūpiyasaṃvohāraṃ [M 1.0112] āvuso samāpajjantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ.

The question: Friend, where was it laid down by the Blessed One that it is an offense entailing relinquishment and confession for one who engages in monetary transactions?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ.

The reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī.

Pucchā – kaṃ āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The question: With reference to whom, friend, and concerning what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – chabbaggiye bhante bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū nānappakārakaṃ rūpiyasaṃvohāraṃ samāpajjiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the group-of-six bhikkhus. Venerable sir, the group-of-six bhikkhus engaged in various kinds of monetary transactions. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that matter.

2. Kosiyavagga, 10. kayavikkaya sikkhāpucchā

2. The Kosiyavagga 10: Inquiry concerning the training rule on buying and selling

Pucchā – nānappakārakaṃ āvuso kayavikkayaṃ āpajjantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ.

The question: Friend, where was it laid down by the Blessed One that it is an offense entailing relinquishment and confession for one who engages in various kinds of buying and selling?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ.

The reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī.

Pucchā – kaṃ āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The question: With reference to whom, friend, and concerning what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – āyasmantaṃ bhante upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante upanando sakyaputto paribbājakena saddhi kayavikkayaṃ samāpajji, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The reply – Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the Venerable Upananda the Sakyan. Venerable sir, the Venerable Upananda the Sakyan engaged in buying and selling with a wanderer. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that matter.

Pucchā – kathaṃ āvuso tattha paṭipajjanto bhikkhu kayavikkayaṃ samāpajjatināma.

The question – Friend, how is a bhikkhu who practices in a certain way said to be engaging in buying and selling?

Vissajjanā – iminā imaṃ dehi iminā imaṃ āhara iminā imaṃ parivattehi iminā imaṃ cetāmehīti ajjhācarati, evaṃ kho bhante paṭipajjanto kayavikkayaṃ samāpajjatināma.

The reply – He acts in such a way, saying, 'Give this for that,' 'Bring this for that,' 'Exchange this for that,' or 'Buy this for that.' Venerable sir, a bhikkhu practicing in this way is said to be engaging in buying and selling.

Pucchā – kathaṃ [M 1.0114] āvuso tattha paṭipajjanto bhikkhu na kayavikkayaṃ samāpajjatināma.

The question – Friend, how is a bhikkhu who practices in a certain way not said to be engaging in buying and selling?

Vissajjanā – yo bhante agghaṃ pucchati, yo ca bhante kappiyakārakassa ācikkhati, yo ca bhante amhākañca idaṃ atthi amhākañca iminā ca iminā ca atthoti bhaṇati, evaṃ kho bhante paṭipajjanto bhikkhu na kayavikkayaṃ samāpajjatināma.

The reply – Venerable sir, one who asks the price, one who informs the steward, and one who says, 'We have this, and we need this and that'—Venerable sir, a bhikkhu practicing in this way is not said to be engaging in buying and selling.

3. Pattavagga, 1. patta sikkhāpucchā

3. The Chapter on Bowls, 1. Inquiry into the Training Rule on Bowls

Pucchā – atirekapattaṃ āvuso dasāhaṃ atikkāmentassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the rule of forfeiture with confession laid down for one who causes an extra bowl to be kept beyond ten days?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī.

Pucchā – kaṃ [M 1.0115] āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, concerning whom and in regard to what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – chabbaggiye bhante bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū atirekapattaṃ dhāresuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the group-of-six monks. Venerable sir, the group-of-six monks kept extra bowls. It was in regard to that matter, venerable sir, that it was laid down.

514 3. Pattavagga, 2. ūnapañcabandhana sikkhāpucchā

514 3. The Chapter on Bowls, 2. Inquiry into the Training Rule on a Bowl with Less Than Five Mends

Pucchā – ūnapañcabandhanena pattena aññaṃ navaṃ pattaṃ cetāpentassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ.

Question: Where was the rule of forfeiture with confession laid down for one who, with a bowl having less than five mends, has another new bowl acquired?

Vissajjanā – sakkesu bhante paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down among the Sakyans.

Pucchā – kaṃ [M 1.0116] āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, concerning whom and in regard to what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – chabbaggiye bhante bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū appamattakenapi bhinnena appamattakenapi khaṇḍena vilikhitamattenapi bahū patte viyyāpesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the group-of-six monks. Venerable sir, the group-of-six monks had many bowls exchanged for being slightly broken, slightly chipped, or merely scratched. It was in regard to that matter, venerable sir, that it was laid down.

Na bhikkhave patto viññāpetabbo, yo viññāpeyya āpatti dukkaṭassa.

Monks, a bowl is not to be requested. Whoever should request one, there is an offense of wrong-doing.

3. Pattavagga 3. bhesajja sikkhāpucchā

3. The Chapter on Bowls, 3. Inquiry into the Training Rule on Medicine

Pucchā – bhesajjāni [M 1.0117] āvuso paṭiggahetvā sattāhaṃ atikkāmentassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the rule of forfeiture with confession laid down for one who, having received medicines, causes them to be kept beyond seven days?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī.

Pucchā – kaṃ āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, concerning whom and in regard to what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sambahule bhante bhikkhū ārabbha ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhū bhesajjāni paṭiggahetvā sattāhaṃ atikkāmesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning a number of monks. Venerable sir, a number of monks, having received medicines, kept them beyond seven days. It was in regard to that matter, venerable sir, that it was laid down.

3. Pattavagga 4. vassikasāṭika sikkhāpucchā

3. The Chapter on Bowls, 4. Inquiry into the Training Rule on the Rains-Cloth

Pucchā – atirekamāse [M 1.0118] sese gimāne āvuso vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyesantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the rule of forfeiture with confession laid down for one who seeks a rains-cloth when more than a month of the hot season remains?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī.

Pucchā – kaṃ [M 1.0119] āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, concerning whom and in regard to what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – chabbaggiye bhante bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū atirekamāse sese gimhāne vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyesiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the group-of-six monks. Venerable sir, the group-of-six monks sought a rains-cloth when more than a month of the hot season remained. It was in regard to that matter, venerable sir, that it was laid down.

3. Pattavagga 5. cīvaraacchindana sikkhāpucchā

3. The Chapter on Bowls, 5. Inquiry into the Training Rule on Taking Back a Robe

Pucchā – bhikkhussa āvuso sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ datvā kupitena anattamanena acchindantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the rule of forfeiture with confession laid down for a bhikkhu who, having himself given a robe, takes it back, being angry and displeased?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī.

Pucchā – kaṃ [M 1.0120] āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, concerning whom and in regard to what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – āyasmantaṃ bhante upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante upanando sakyaputto bhikkhussa sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ datvā kupito anattamano acchindi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the venerable Upananda, the Sakyan son. Venerable sir, the venerable Upananda, the Sakyan son, having himself given a robe to a bhikkhu, angrily and displeased, seized it back. It was on that ground, venerable sir, that it was laid down.”

3. Pattavaggo, 6. suttaviññatti sikkhāpucchā

3. Chapter on Bowls, 6. Inquiry into the Training Rule concerning Requesting Thread

Pucchā – sāmaṃ āvuso suttaṃ viññāpetvā tantavāyehi cīvaraṃ vāyāpentassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the Nissaggiya Pācittiya rule laid down concerning one who, having himself requested thread, causes weavers to weave a robe?”

Vissajjanā – rājagahe bhante paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down in Rājagaha.”

Pucchā – kaṃ āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, concerning whom and on what ground was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – chabbaggiye bhante bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū sāmaṃ suttaṃ viññāpetvā tantavāyehi cīvaraṃ vāyāpesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the group-of-six monks. Venerable sir, the group-of-six monks, having themselves requested thread, caused weavers to weave a robe. It was on that ground, venerable sir, that it was laid down.”

3. Pattavagga, 7. mahāpesakāra sikkhāpucchā

3. Chapter on Bowls, 7. Inquiry into the Training Rule concerning the Great Weaver

Pucchā – pubbe [M 1.0122] appavāritassa āvuso aññātakassa gahapatikassa tantavāye upasaṅkamitvā cīvare vikappaṃ āpajjantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the Nissaggiya Pācittiya rule laid down concerning one who, not previously invited, approaches the weavers of an unrelated householder and makes a specification concerning a robe?”

Vissajjanā – sāvatthi yaṃ bhante paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down in Sāvatthī.”

Pucchā – kaṃ [M 1.0123] āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, concerning whom and on what ground was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – āyasmantaṃ bhante upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante upanando sakyaputto pubbe appavārito aññātakassa gahapatikassa tantavāye upasaṅkamitvā cīvare vikappaṃ āpajji, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the venerable Upananda, the Sakyan son. Venerable sir, the venerable Upananda, the Sakyan son, not previously invited, approached the weavers of an unrelated householder and made a specification concerning a robe. It was on that ground, venerable sir, that it was laid down.”

3. Pattavaggo, 8. accekacīvara sikkhāpucchā

3. Chapter on Bowls, 8. Inquiry into the Training Rule concerning the Emergency Robe

Pucchā – acceka cīvaraṃ āvuso paṭiggahetvā cīvarakālasamayaṃ atikkāmentassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the offense of relinquishment and expiation laid down for one who, having accepted an emergency robe, lets the robe season lapse?”

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī.”

Pucchā – kaṃ [M 1.0124] āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, concerning whom and on what ground was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – sambahule bhante bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhū accekacīvaraṃ paṭiggahetvā cīvarakālasamayaṃ atikkāmesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning several bhikkhus. Venerable sir, several bhikkhus, having accepted an emergency robe, let the robe season lapse. It was on that ground, venerable sir, that it was laid down.”

3. Pattavaggo, 9. sāsaṅka sikkhāpucchā

3. Chapter on Bowls, 9. Inquiry into the Training Rule concerning a Dangerous Place

Pucchā – tiṇṇaṃ āvuso cīvarānaṃ aññataraṃ cīvaraṃ antaraghare nikkhipitvā atirekachārattaṃ vippavasantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the offense of relinquishment and expiation laid down for one who, having left one of the three robes within an inhabited area, stays away for more than six nights?”

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī.”

Pucchā – kaṃ [M 1.0125] āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, concerning whom and on what ground was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – sambahule bhante bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhū tiṇṇaṃ cīvarānaṃ aññataraṃ cīvaraṃ antaraghare nikkhipitvā atirekachārattaṃ vippavasiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning several bhikkhus. Venerable sir, several bhikkhus, having left one of the three robes within an inhabited area, stayed away for more than six nights. It was on that ground, venerable sir, that it was laid down.”

3. Pattavaggo, 10. pariṇata sikkhāpucchā

3. Chapter on Bowls, 10. Inquiry into the Training Rule concerning Diverting to Oneself

Pucchā – yaṃ tena āvuso bhagavatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhena jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ atthano pariṇāmentassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the offense of relinquishment and expiation laid down by the Blessed One, the Arahant, the Perfectly Self-Enlightened One, who sees and knows, for one who knowingly diverts to himself a gain intended for the Sangha?”

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī.”

Pucchā – kaṃ [M 1.0126] āvuso ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, concerning whom and on what ground was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – chabbaggiye bhante bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ attano pariṇāmesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the group-of-six bhikkhus. Venerable sir, the group-of-six bhikkhus knowingly diverted to themselves a gain intended for the Sangha. It was on that ground, venerable sir, that it was laid down.”

Pucchā – jānaṃ [M 1.0127] āvuso saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ attano pariṇāmento, kati āpattiyo āpajjati.

Question: “Friend, how many offenses does one incur by knowingly diverting to oneself a gain intended for the Sangha?”

Vissajjanā – jānaṃ bhante saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ attano pariṇāmento dve āpattiyo āpajjati, pariṇāmeti payoge dukkaṭaṃ, pariṇāmite nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ, jānaṃ bhante saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ attano pariṇāmento imā dve āpattiyo āpajjati.

Reply: “Venerable sir, by knowingly diverting to oneself a gain intended for the Sangha, one incurs two offenses: a dukkaṭa offense in the attempt to divert, and an offense of relinquishment and expiation when the diversion is completed. Venerable sir, by knowingly diverting to oneself a gain intended for the Sangha, one incurs these two offenses.”

Pucchā – kassa [M 1.0128] āvuso vacanaṃ.

Question: “Friend, whose word is this?”

Vissajjanā – bhagavato bhante vacanaṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa.

Reply: “Venerable sir, this is the word of the Blessed One, the Arahant, the Perfectly Self-Enlightened One.”

Pucchā – kenāvuso ābhataṃ.

Question: Friend, by whom was it brought?

Vissajjanā – paramparāya bhante ābhataṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it has been handed down by tradition.

Pucchā – atthi nukho āvuso tattha kocipi viraddhadoso.

Question: Friend, is there any fault of omission there?

Vissajjanā – natthi bhante.

Reply: Venerable sir, there is none.

Saṃgāyanatthāya ñāpanaṃ

Announcement for the purpose of the Recitation

Suṇātu [M 1.0129] me bhante saṅgho, ahaṃ bhante imesaṃ soḷasannaṃ nissaggiyapācittiyasikkhāpadānaṃ nidānādīni anekāni pucchitabbāni āyasmantaṃ vicittasārābhivaṃsaṃ tipiṭakadhara dhammabhaṇḍāgārikaṃ pucchiṃ, so ca puṭṭho puṭṭho vissajjeti, itihidaṃ bhante sikkhāpadasañjātaṃ nimmalaṃ su parisuddhaṃ tasseva bhagavato vacanaṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa, tasmā yathā pure mahākassapādayo mahāthera varā porāṇasaṃgītikārā brahmacariyasaṅkhātassa buddhasāsanassa ciraṭṭhitiyā bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya lokānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya devamanussānaṃ dhammavinayaṃ saṃgāyiṃsuceva anusaṃgāyiṃsuca, evameva mayampi dāni chaṭṭhasaṃgīti mahādhammasabhāpariyāpannā imāni sikkhāpadāni yathānuppattaṭṭhānato paṭṭhāya ekato gaṇasajjhāyaṃ katvā saṃgāyeyyāma.

Venerable sirs, may the Sangha listen to me. Venerable sirs, regarding these sixteen training rules entailing relinquishment and expiation, I asked the Venerable Vicittasārābhivaṃsa, the Bearer of the Tipiṭaka and Keeper of the Dhamma Treasury, many questions about their origins and other details. And he, being asked, answered each question. Thus, venerable sirs, this body of training rules, arisen, is stainless and perfectly pure, being the very word of the Blessed One, the Arahant, the Perfectly Self-Enlightened One. Therefore, just as in the past the great elder Mahākassapa and other noble ancient compilers recited and re-recited the Dhamma and Vinaya for the long-lasting of the holy life, which is the Buddha’s Dispensation, for the welfare of the many, for the happiness of the many, out of compassion for the world, for the benefit, welfare, and happiness of devas and humans—so too, we now, gathered in this Sixth Great Dhamma Council, should recite these training rules together from the place where they occur, having first rehearsed them collectively.

Pācittiyapāḷi

The Pācittiya Pāli

1. Musāvādavagga, 1. musāvāda sikkhāpucchā

1. The Chapter on False Speech, 1. Question on the Training Rule on False Speech

Pucchā – yaṃ tena āvuso bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhena sampajānamusāvāde pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the pācittiya offense concerning intentional lying laid down by that Blessed One, who knows and sees, the Arahant, the Perfectly Self-Enlightened One? With reference to whom was it laid down? Concerning what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ, hatthakaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, hatthako bhante sakyaputto titthiyehi saddhiṃ sallapanto avajānitvā paṭijānāti paṭijānitvā avajānāti, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī. It was laid down with reference to Hatthaka the Sakyan. Venerable sir, Hatthaka the Sakyan, while conversing with adherents of other sects, would deny what he had acknowledged and acknowledge what he had denied. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that matter.

1. Musāvādavaggo, 2-3. omasavāda, pesuñña sikkhāpucchā

1. The Chapter on False Speech, 2-3. Questions on the Training Rules on Abusive Speech and Slanderous Speech

Pucchā – omasavāde pācittiyañca āvuso bhikkhupesuññe pācittiyañca bhagavatā katthapaññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where were the Pācittiya rules concerning abusive speech and slander among bhikkhus laid down by the Blessed One? Concerning whom, in what incident were they established?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ [M 1.0132] bhante paññattaṃ, chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ bhaṇḍantā omasiṃsu tasmiṃca vatthusmiṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū bhikkhūnaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpannānaṃ pesuññaṃ upasaṃhariṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, they were laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the group-of-six bhikkhus. Venerable sir, the group-of-six bhikkhus, while quarreling with other bhikkhus, reviled them; it was laid down concerning that matter. And, venerable sir, the group-of-six bhikkhus brought tale-bearing against bhikkhus who were quarreling, contending, and disputing; it was laid down concerning that matter.

1. Musāvādavagga, 4. padasodhamma sikkhāpucchā

1. The Chapter on False Speech, 4. Question on the Training Rule Regarding Reciting Dhamma Word by Word

Pucchā – anupasampannaṃ āvuso padaso vācentassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the Pācittiya rule concerning teaching an unordained person word by word laid down? Concerning whom, and in what incident was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ, chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū upāsake padaso dhammaṃ vācesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī. It was laid down concerning the group-of-six bhikkhus. Venerable sir, the group-of-six bhikkhus taught the Dhamma word by word to lay followers. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that matter.

1. Musāvādavaggo 5. sahaseyya sikkhāpucchā

1. The Chapter on False Speech, 5. Question on the Training Rule Regarding Lying Down Together

Pucchā – anupasampannena āvuso uttaridirattatirattaṃ sahaseyyaṃ kappentassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the Pācittiya rule laid down for one who shares a sleeping place with an unordained person for more than two or three nights? Concerning whom, and in what incident was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – āḷaviyaṃ bhante paññattaṃ, sambahule bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhū anupasampannena sahaseyyaṃ kappesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Āḷavī. It was laid down concerning a number of bhikkhus. Venerable sir, a number of bhikkhus shared a sleeping place with an unordained person. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that matter.

Pucchā – kesaṃ [M 1.0134] āvuso ettha anupasampannena sahaseyyaṃ kappentānampi anāpatti hoti.

Question: Friend, for whom is there no offense in this case, even when sharing a sleeping place with an unordained person?

Vissajjanā – ekādasannaṃ bhante puggalānaṃ anupasampannena sahaseyyaṃ kappentānaṃ anāpatti, dvetisso rattiyo vasatī, ūnakadve tisso rattiyo vasati, dverattiyo vasitvā tatiyāyarattiyā purāruṇā nikkhamitvā puna vasati, sabbacchanne sabbaaparicchanne, sabbaparicchanne sabbaacchanne, yebhuyyena acchanne yebhuyyena aparicchanne, anupasampanne nipanne bhikkhu nisīdati, bhikkhunipanne anupasampanno nisīdati, ubhovā nisīdanti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassa, imesaṃkho bhante ekādasannaṃ puggalānaṃ anupasampannena sahaseyyaṃ kappentānaṃ anāpatti.

Reply: Venerable sir, there is no offense for eleven kinds of persons when sharing a sleeping place with an unordained person: one who stays for two or three nights; one who stays for less than two or three nights; one who stays for two nights, and on the third night leaves before dawn and then stays again; one in a fully roofed and fully unenclosed space; one in a fully enclosed and fully unroofed space; one in a mostly unroofed and mostly unenclosed space; when an unordained person lies down and a bhikkhu sits; when a bhikkhu lies down and an unordained person sits; when both sit; for one who is insane; for the first offender. Venerable sir, for these eleven kinds of persons, there is no offense when sharing a sleeping place with an unordained person.

1. Musāvādavaggo, 6. dutiya sahaseyya sikkhāpucchā

1. The Chapter on False Speech, 6. Second Question on the Training Rule Regarding Lying Down Together

Pucchā – mātugāmena [M 1.0135] āvuso sahaseyyaṃ kappentassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the Pācittiya rule for one who shares a sleeping place with a woman laid down? Concerning whom, and in what incident was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante anuruddho mātugāmena sahaseyyaṃ kappesi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the Venerable Anuruddha. Venerable sir, the Venerable Anuruddha shared a sleeping place with a woman. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that matter.

1. Musāvādavaggo, 7. dhammadesanā sikkhāpucchā

1. The Chapter on False Speech, 7. Question on the Training Rule Regarding Teaching the Dhamma

Pucchā – mātugāmassa [M 1.0136] āvuso uttari chappañcavācāhi dhammaṃ desentassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the Pācittiya rule laid down for one who teaches the Dhamma to a woman with more than five or six sentences? Concerning whom, and in what incident was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante udāyī mātugāmassa dhammaṃdeseti, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the Venerable Udāyī. Venerable sir, the Venerable Udāyī taught the Dhamma to a woman. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that matter.

1. Musāvādavaggo, 8. bhūtārocana sikkhāpucchā

1. The Chapter on False Speech, 8. Question on the Training Rule Regarding Revealing a True Attainment

Pucchā – anupasampannassa [M 1.0137] āvuso uttarimanussadhammaṃ bhūtaṃ ārocentassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the Pācittiya rule laid down for one who reports a true superhuman state to an unordained person? Concerning whom, and in what incident was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – vesāliyaṃ bhante vaggumudātīriye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, vaggumudātīriyā bhante bhikkhū gihīnaṃ aññamaññassa uttarimanussadhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Vesālī concerning the bhikkhus on the bank of the Vaggumudā river. Venerable sir, the bhikkhus on the bank of the Vaggumudā praised each other’s superhuman states to the laypeople. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that matter.

1. Musāvādavaggo, 9. duṭṭhullārocana sikkhāpucchā

1. The Chapter on False Speech, 9. Question on the Training Rule Concerning Revealing a Grave Offense.

Pucchā – bhikkhussa [M 1.0138] āvuso duṭṭhullāpattiṃ anupasampannassa ārocentassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the pācittiya offense laid down for one who reports a bhikkhu’s grave offense to an unordained person? Concerning whom, and in what incident was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū bhikkhussa duṭṭhullāpattiṃ anupasampannassa ārocesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the group-of-six bhikkhus. Venerable sir, the group-of-six bhikkhus reported a bhikkhu’s grave offense to an unordained person. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that matter.”

1. Musāvādavaggo, 10. pathavīkhaṇana sikkhāpucchā

1. The Chapter on False Speech, 10. Question on the Training Rule Concerning Digging the Earth.

Pucchā – pathaviṃkhaṇantassa [M 1.0139] āvuso pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was a pācittiya offense for one who digs the earth laid down? Concerning whom was it laid down? On what occasion was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – āḷaviyaṃ bhante āḷavike bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, āḷavikā bhante bhikkhū pathaviṃkhaṇiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, at Āḷavī it was laid down concerning the monks of Āḷavī. Venerable sir, the monks of Āḷavī dug the earth. Venerable sir, on that occasion it was laid down.”

2. Bhūtagāmavaggo, 1. bhūtagāma sikkhāpucchā

2. The Chapter on Plants, 1. Question on the Training Rule Concerning Plants.

Pucchā – bhūtagāmapātabyatāya [M 1.0140] pācittiyaṃ āvuso bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was a pācittiya offense for destroying plant life laid down by the Blessed One? Concerning whom was it laid down? On what occasion was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – āḷaviyaṃ bhante āḷavike bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, āḷavikā bhante bhikkhū rukkhaṃ chindiṃ su, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, at Āḷavī it was laid down concerning the monks of Āḷavī. Venerable sir, the monks of Āḷavī cut down a tree. Venerable sir, on that occasion it was laid down.”

2. Bhūtagāmavagga, 2. aññavādaka sikkhāpucchā

2. The Chapter on Plants, 2. Question on the Training Rule Concerning Evasive Speech.

Pucchā – aññavādake vihesake pācittiyaṃ āvuso bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was a pācittiya offense for one who speaks evasively and harasses laid down by the Blessed One? Concerning whom was it laid down? On what occasion was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – kosambiyaṃ [M 1.0141] bhante āyasmantaṃ channaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante channo saṅghamajjhe āpattiyā anuyuñjīyamāno aññenaññaṃ paṭicari, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, at Kosambī it was laid down concerning the Venerable Channa. Venerable sir, the Venerable Channa, being questioned about an offense in the midst of the Sangha, replied evasively. Venerable sir, on that occasion it was laid down.”

2. Bhūtagāmavagga, 3. ujjhāpanaka sikkhāpucchā

2. The Chapter on Plants, 3. Question on the Training Rule Concerning Scandalizing.

Pucchā – ujjhāpanake khiyyanake pācittiyaṃ āvuso bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was a pācittiya offense for one who scandalizes and disparages laid down by the Blessed One? Concerning whom was it laid down? On what occasion was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – rājagahe bhante mettiyabhūmajake bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, mettiyabhūmajakā bhante bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mallaputtaṃ bhikkhū ujjhāpesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, at Rājagaha it was laid down concerning the Mettiya-Bhūmajaka monks. Venerable sir, the Mettiya-Bhūmajaka monks scandalized the Venerable Dabba Mallaputta to the monks. Venerable sir, on that occasion it was laid down.”

2. Bhūtagāmavagga, 4. paṭhama senāsana sikkhāpucchā

2. The Chapter on Plants, 4. First Question on the Training Rule Concerning Lodgings.

Pucchā – saṅghikaṃ [M 1.0142] āvuso mañcaṃvā pīṭhaṃvā bhisiṃvā kocchaṃvā ajjhokāse santharitvā anuddharitvā anāpucchā pakkamantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was a pācittiya offense laid down for one who, having spread out a Sangha bed, chair, mattress, or cushion in the open air, departs without taking it up and without asking leave? Concerning whom was it laid down? On what occasion was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante sambahule bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhū saṅghikaṃ senāsanaṃ ajjhokāse santharitvā anuddharitvā anāpucchā pakkamiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, at Sāvatthī it was laid down concerning several monks. Venerable sir, several monks spread out a Sangha lodging in the open air and departed without taking it up and without asking leave. Venerable sir, on that occasion it was laid down.”

2. Bhūtagāmavagga, 5. dutiya senāsana sikkhāpucchā

2. The Chapter on Plants, 5. Second Question on the Training Rule Concerning Lodgings.

Pucchā – saṅghike [M 1.0143] āvuso vihāre seyyaṃ santharitvā anuddharitvā anāpucchā pakkamantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was a pācittiya offense laid down for one who, having spread out bedding in a Sangha dwelling, departs without taking it up and without asking leave? Concerning whom was it laid down? On what occasion was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante sattarasavaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, sattarasavaggiyā bhante bhikkhū saṅghike vihāre seyyaṃ santharitvā anuddharitvā anāpucchā pakkamiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, at Sāvatthī it was laid down concerning the monks of the group of seventeen. Venerable sir, the monks of the group of seventeen spread out bedding in a Sangha dwelling and departed without taking it up and without asking leave. Venerable sir, on that occasion it was laid down.”

Pucchā – kassa [M 1.0144] āvuso vacanaṃ.

Question: “Whose word is this, friend?”

Vissajjanā – bhagavatā bhante vacanaṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it is the word of the Blessed One, the Arahant, the Perfectly Self-Enlightened One.”

Pucchā – kenāvuso ābhataṃ.

Question: “Friend, by whom was it brought?”

Vissajjanā – paramparāya bhante ābhataṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it has been handed down through tradition.”

Pucchā – atthi [M 1.0145] nukho āvuso ettha kocipi viraddhadoso.

Question: “Friend, is there anyone here with a fault of transgression?”

Vissajjanā – natthi bhante.

Reply: “Venerable sir, there is none.”

Saṅghassa ñāpanaṃ

The Notification to the Sangha.

Suṇātu me bhante saṅgho, ahaṃ bhante āyasmantaṃ vicittasārābhivaṃsaṃ imesaṃ pannarasannaṃ sikkhāpadānaṃ nidānādīni pucchitabbāni anekāni ṭhānāni pucchiṃ, so ca puṭṭho puṭṭho vissajjeti, iti hidaṃ bhante nimmalaṃ suparisuddhaṃ tasseva bhagavato vacanaṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa, tasmā yathāpure mahākassapādayo mahātheravarā porāṇasaṃgītikārā brahmacariyassa ciraṭṭhitiyā bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya lonukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya devamanussānaṃ dhammavinayaṃ saṃgāyiṃsuceva anusaṃgāyiṃsuca, evameva mayampi dāni chaṭṭhasaṃgītimahādhammasabhā pariyāpannā yathānuppaṭṭhānato paṭṭhāya imāni sikkhāpadāni ekato gaṇasajjhāyaṃ katvā saṃgāyeyyāma.

May the Sangha, venerable sirs, listen to me. Venerable sirs, I questioned the Venerable Vicittasārābhivaṃsa concerning the origins and other details of these fifteen training rules, asking about many points. And he, being questioned, answered each time. Thus, venerable sirs, this is indeed the immaculate and perfectly pure word of that Blessed One, the Arahant, the Perfectly Self-Enlightened One. Therefore, just as formerly the great elder Mahākassapa and others, the excellent great elders, the ancient reciters of the council, recited and rehearsed the Dhamma and Vinaya for the long standing of the holy life, for the welfare of the many, for the happiness of the many, out of compassion for the world, for the benefit, welfare, and happiness of gods and humans—even so, we too, now, who are included in the Sixth Great Dhamma Council, beginning from the established order, should recite these training rules together in a group recitation.

2. Bhūtagāmavagga, 6. anupakhajja sikkhāpucchā

2. The Chapter on Plants, 6. Question on the Training Rule Concerning Encroaching.

Pucchā – yaṃ [M 1.0146] tena āvuso…pe… sammāsambuddhena saṅghike vihāre jānaṃ pubbupagataṃ bhikkhuṃ anupakhajja seyyaṃ kappentassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense entailing confession laid down by the Blessed One, the Perfectly Self-Enlightened One, regarding a monk who, knowing, lies down encroaching upon a monk who arrived earlier in a monastic dwelling belonging to the Sangha? Concerning whom and on what occasion was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū there bhikkhū anupakhajja seyyaṃ kappesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Answer: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the group of six monks. Venerable sir, the group of six monks lay down encroaching upon the elder monks. Venerable sir, it was laid down on that occasion.

2. Bhūtagāmavagga, 7. nikaḍḍhana sikkhāpucchā

2. The Chapter on Plants, 7. Inquiry concerning the training rule on dragging out.

Pucchā – bhikkhuṃ [M 1.0147] āvuso kupitena anattamanena saṅghikā vihārā nikkaḍḍhentassa pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense entailing confession laid down by the Blessed One for one who, angry and displeased, drags a monk out of a dwelling belonging to the Sangha? Concerning whom and on what occasion was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū kupitā anattamanā bhikkhū saṅghikāvihārā nikkaḍḍhiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Answer: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the group of six monks. Venerable sir, the group of six monks, angry and displeased, dragged monks out of a dwelling belonging to the Sangha. Venerable sir, it was laid down on that occasion.

2. Bhūtagāmavagga, 8. vehāsakuṭi sikkhāpucchā

2. The Chapter on Plants, 8. Inquiry concerning the training rule on an upper story dwelling.

Pucchā – uparivehāsakuṭiyā āvuso āhaccapādakaṃ mañcaṃvā pīṭhaṃvā abhinisīdantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense entailing confession laid down for one who sits or lies down on a bed or bench with detachable legs in an upper story dwelling? Concerning whom and on what occasion was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ [M 1.0148] bhante aññataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, aññataro bhante bhikkhu saṅghike vihāre uparivehāsa kuṭiyā āhaccapādakaṃ mañcaṃ sahasā abhinisīdi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Answer: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning a certain monk. Venerable sir, a certain monk forcefully sat down on a bed with detachable legs in an upper story dwelling belonging to the Sangha. Venerable sir, it was laid down on that occasion.

2. Bhūtagāmavagga, 9. mahallakavihāra sikkhāpucchā

2. The Chapter on Plants, 9. Inquiry concerning the training rule on a large dwelling.

Pucchā – dvatti pariyāye āvuso adhiṭṭhahitvā tatuttari adhiṭṭhahantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense entailing confession laid down for one who, having applied two or three layers, applies more beyond that? Concerning whom and on what occasion was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – kosambiyaṃ bhante āyasmantaṃ channattheraṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante chando katapariyositaṃ vihāraṃ punappunaṃ chādāpesi punappunaṃ lepāpesi, atibhārito vihāro paripati, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Answer: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Kosambi concerning the Venerable Elder Channa. Venerable sir, the Venerable Channa repeatedly had a dwelling that was completed re-roofed and repeatedly had it plastered. The dwelling, being overloaded, collapsed. Venerable sir, it was laid down on that occasion.

2. Bhūtagāmavagga, 10. sappāṇaka sikkhāpucchā

2. The Chapter on Plants, 10. Inquiry concerning the training rule on water containing living beings.

Pucchā – jānaṃ [M 1.0149] āvuso sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ tiṇaṃvā mattikaṃvā siñcantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense entailing confession laid down for one who, knowing that water contains living beings, sprinkles it on grass or clay? Concerning whom and on what occasion was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – āḷaviyaṃ bhante āḷavike bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, āḷavikā bhante bhikkhū jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ tiṇampi mattikampi siñciṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Answer: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Āḷavī concerning the monks of Āḷavī. Venerable sir, the monks of Āḷavī, knowing, sprinkled grass and clay with water containing living beings. Venerable sir, it was laid down on that occasion.

3. Ovādavagga, 1. ovāda sikkhāpucchā

3. The Chapter on Exhortation, 1. Inquiry concerning the training rule on exhortation.

Pucchā – asammatena [M 1.0150] āvuso bhikkhuniyo ovadantassa pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense entailing confession laid down by the Blessed One regarding a monk who, unappointed, exhorts nuns? Concerning whom and on what occasion was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū asammatā bhikkhuniyo ovadiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Answer: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the group of six monks. Venerable sir, the group of six monks, not being appointed, exhorted the nuns. Venerable sir, it was laid down on that occasion.

3. Ovādavagga, 2. atthaṅgata sikkhāpucchā

3. The Chapter on Exhortation, 2. Inquiry concerning the training rule on exhorting after sunset.

Pucchā – atthaṅgate āvuso sūriye bhikkhuniyo ovadantassa pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense entailing confession laid down by the Blessed One regarding exhorting nuns after the sun has set? Concerning whom and on what occasion was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante āyasmantaṃ cūḷapanthakaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante cūḷapanthako atthaṅgate sūriye bhikkhuniyo ovadi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Answer: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the Venerable Cūḷapanthaka. Venerable sir, the Venerable Cūḷapanthaka exhorted the nuns after the sun had set. Venerable sir, it was laid down on that occasion.

3. Ovādavagga, 3-4. bhikkhunupassaya, āmisa sikkhāpucchā

3. The Chapter on Exhortation, 3-4. Inquiry concerning the training rule on a nuns' residence and material gain.

Pucchā – bhikkhunupassayaṃ [M 1.0151] āvuso upasaṅkamitvā bhikkhuniyo ovadantassaca āmisahetu therā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyo ovadantīti bhaṇantassaca pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense entailing confession laid down by the Blessed One regarding one who goes to a nuns' residence and exhorts nuns, and regarding one who says, 'Elder monks exhort nuns for the sake of material gain'? Concerning whom and on what occasion was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – bhikkhunupassayaṃ upasaṅkamitvā bhikkhuniyo ovadantassa pācittiyaṃ bhante sakkesu chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū bhikkhunupassayaṃ upasaṅkamitvā bhikkhuniyo ovadiṃsu, āmisahetu therā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyo ovadantīti bhaṇantassa pācittiyaṃ sāvatthiyaṃ chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū āmisahetu therā bhikkhū [M 1.0152] bhikkhuniyo ovadantīti bhaṇiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Answer: Venerable sir, the offense entailing confession for approaching a nuns' residence and exhorting nuns was laid down concerning the group of six monks among the Sakyans. Venerable sir, the group of six monks approached a nuns' residence and exhorted the nuns. The offense entailing confession for saying, 'Elder monks exhort nuns for the sake of material gain,' was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the group of six monks. Venerable sir, the group of six monks said, 'Elder monks exhort nuns for the sake of material gain.' Venerable sir, it was laid down on that occasion.

3. Ovādavagga, 5. cīvaradāna sikkhāpucchā

3. The Chapter on Exhortation, 5. Inquiry concerning the training rule on giving a robe.

Pucchā – aññātikāya [M 1.0153] āvuso bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ dentassa pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense entailing confession laid down by the Blessed One for one who gives a robe to an unrelated nun? Concerning whom and on what occasion was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante aññataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, aññataro bhante bhikkhu aññātikāya bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ adāsi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Answer: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning a certain monk. Venerable sir, a certain monk gave a robe to an unrelated nun. Venerable sir, it was laid down on that occasion.

3. Ovādavagga, 6. cīvarasibbana sikkhāpucchā

3. The Chapter on Exhortation, 6. Inquiry concerning the training rule on sewing a robe.

Pucchā – aññātikāya āvuso bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ sibbentassa pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense entailing confession laid down by the Blessed One for one who sews a robe for an unrelated nun? Concerning whom and on what occasion was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante udāyī aññātikāya bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ sibbesi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Answer: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the Venerable Udāyī. Venerable sir, the Venerable Udāyī sewed a robe for an unrelated nun. Venerable sir, it was laid down on that occasion.

3. Ovādavagga, 7. saṃvidhāna sikkhāpucchā

3. The Chapter on Exhortation, 7. Inquiry concerning the training rule on making arrangements.

Pucchā – bhikkhunayā [M 1.0154] āvuso saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya ekaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipajjantassa pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question – Friend, where was the offense entailing expiation laid down by the Blessed One for one who, having made an arrangement with a bhikkhunī, travels on the same road? Concerning whom was it laid down? On what occasion was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya ekaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipajjiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply – Venerable Sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the group-of-six bhikkhus. Venerable Sir, the group-of-six bhikkhus, having made an arrangement with bhikkhunīs, traveled on the same road. Venerable Sir, it was laid down on that occasion.

3. Ovādavagga, 8. nāvābhiruhana sikkhāpucchā

3. The Chapter on Exhortation, 8. Inquiry about the Training Rule on Boarding a Boat

Pucchā – bhikkhuniyā [M 1.0155] āvuso saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya ekaṃ nāvaṃ abhiruhantassa pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question – Friend, where was the offense entailing expiation laid down by the Blessed One for one who, having made an arrangement with a bhikkhunī, boards a single boat? Concerning whom was it laid down? On what occasion was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū bhikkhūnīhi saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya ekaṃ nāvaṃ abhiruhiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply – Venerable Sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the group-of-six bhikkhus. Venerable Sir, the group-of-six bhikkhus, having made an arrangement with bhikkhunīs, boarded a single boat. Venerable Sir, it was laid down on that occasion.

3. Ovādavagga, 9. paripācita sikkhāpucchā

3. The Chapter on Exhortation, 9. Inquiry about the Training Rule on Specially Prepared Food

Pucchā – jānaṃ āvuso bhikkhuniparipācitaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñjantassa pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question – Friend, where was the offense entailing expiation laid down by the Blessed One for one who eats almsfood knowing it to have been specially prepared by a bhikkhunī? Concerning whom was it laid down? On what occasion was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – rājagahe bhante devadattaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, devadatto bhante jānaṃ bhikkhuniparipācitaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñji, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply – Venerable Sir, it was laid down at Rājagaha concerning Devadatta. Venerable Sir, Devadatta knowingly ate almsfood specially prepared by a bhikkhunī. Venerable Sir, it was laid down on that occasion.

3. Ovādavagga, 10. rahonisajja sikkhāpucchā

3. The Chapter on Exhortation, 10. Inquiry about the Training Rule on Sitting in Private

Pucchā – bhikkhuniyā [M 1.0156] saddhiṃ āvuso eko ekāya raho nisajjaṃ kappentassa pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question – Friend, where was the offense entailing expiation laid down by the Blessed One for one who sits alone in private with a bhikkhunī? Concerning whom was it laid down? On what occasion was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante udāyī bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ eko ekāya raho nisajjaṃ kappesi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply – Venerable Sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the Venerable Udāyī. Venerable Sir, the Venerable Udāyī sat alone in private with a bhikkhunī. Venerable Sir, it was laid down on that occasion.

4. Bhojanavagga, 1. āvasathapiṇḍa sikkhāpucchā

4. The Chapter on Meals, 1. Inquiry about the Training Rule on Almsfood from a Lodging Place

Pucchā – tatuttari [M 1.0157] āvuso āvasathapiṇḍaṃ bhuñjantassa pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question – Friend, where was the offense entailing expiation laid down by the Blessed One for one who eats almsfood from a lodging place in excess of that? Concerning whom was it laid down? On what occasion was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū anuvasitvā anuvasitvā āvasathapiṇḍaṃ paribhuñjiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply – Venerable Sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the group-of-six bhikkhus. Venerable Sir, the group-of-six bhikkhus, staying day after day, consumed almsfood from a lodging place. Venerable Sir, it was laid down on that occasion.

4. Bhojanavagga, 2. gaṇabhojana sikkhāpucchā

4. The Chapter on Meals, 2. Inquiry about the Training Rule on Group Meals

Pucchā – gaṇabhojane pācittiyaṃ āvuso bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question – Friend, where was the offense entailing expiation concerning a group meal laid down by the Blessed One? Concerning whom was it laid down? On what occasion was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – rājagahe bhante devadattaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, devadatto bhante sapariso kulesu viññāpetvā bhuñji, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply – Venerable Sir, it was laid down at Rājagaha concerning Devadatta. Venerable Sir, Devadatta, together with his retinue, having requested from families, ate. Venerable Sir, it was laid down on that occasion.

4. Bhojanavagga 3. paramparabhojana sikkhāpucchā

4. The Chapter on Meals, 3. Inquiry about the Training Rule on Successive Eating

Pucchā – parampara [M 1.0158] bhojane pācittiyaṃ āvuso bhagavatā kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question – Friend, where was the offense entailing expiation concerning successive eating laid down by the Blessed One? Concerning whom was it laid down? On what occasion was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – vesāliyaṃ bhante sambahule bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhū aññatra nimantitā aññatra bhuñjiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply – Venerable Sir, it was laid down at Vesālī concerning several bhikkhus. Venerable Sir, several bhikkhus, having been invited to one place, ate in another. Venerable Sir, it was laid down on that occasion.

4. Bhojanavagga, 4. kāṇamātu sikkhāpucchā

4. The Chapter on Meals, 4. Inquiry about the Training Rule concerning Kāṇamātā

Pucchā – dvattipattapūraṃ [M 1.0159] pūvaṃ paṭiggahetvā tatuttari paṭiggaṇhantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question – Where was the offense entailing expiation laid down for one who, having accepted two or three bowlfuls of cakes, accepts more than that? Concerning whom was it laid down? On what occasion was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante sambahule bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhū na mattaṃ jānitvā pūvaṃ paṭiggahesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply – Venerable Sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning several bhikkhus. Venerable Sir, several bhikkhus accepted cakes without knowing moderation. Venerable Sir, it was laid down on that occasion.

Pucchā – kassa āvuso vacanaṃ.

Question – Whose word is this, friend?

Vissajjanā – bhagavato bhante vacanaṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa.

Reply – Venerable Sir, it is the word of the Blessed One, the Arahant, the Perfectly Self-Enlightened One.

Pucchā – kenāvuso ābhataṃ.

Question – By whom, friend, was it brought?

Vissajjanā – paramparāya bhante ābhataṃ.

Reply – Venerable Sir, it has been handed down through tradition.

Pucchā – atthi nukho āvuso ettha kocipi viraddhadoso.

Question – Friend, is there anyone here at fault for transgression?

Vissajjanā – natthi bhante.

Reply – Venerable Sir, there is none.

4. Bhojanavagga, 5. paṭhamapavāraṇā sikkhāpucchā

4. The Chapter on Meals, 5. Inquiry about the Training Rule concerning the First Refusal

Pucchā – yaṃ [M 1.0160] tena sāvuso…pe… sammāsambuddhena bhuttāvinā parivātena anatirittaṃ khādanīyaṃvā bhojanīyaṃvā bhuñjantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The Question – That which, friend, was laid down by the Blessed One... the Perfectly Enlightened One: for one who, having eaten and having been invited to stop, eats food that is not a leftover, either chewable or staple, there is a pācittiya offense. Where was it laid down? Concerning whom? In what incident was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante sambahule bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhū bhuttāvī pavāritā aññatra bhuñjiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The Reply – Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning several monks at Sāvatthī. Venerable sir, several monks, having eaten and been invited to stop, ate elsewhere. Venerable sir, it was laid down in that incident.

4. Bhojanavagga, 6. dutiya pavāraṇā sikkhāpucchā

4. The Chapter on Food, 6. Question on the Training Rule concerning the Second Invitation

Pucchā – bhikkhuṃ [M 1.0161] āvuso bhuttāviṃ pavāritaṃ anatirittena khādanīyena bhojanīyena abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavārentassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The Question – Friend, where was the pācittiya offense laid down for one who invites a monk—who has eaten and been invited to stop—to partake of food that is not a leftover, either chewable or staple? Concerning whom was it laid down? In what incident was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante aññataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, aññataro bhante bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ bhuttāviṃ pavāritaṃ anatirittena bhojanīyena abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāresi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The Reply – Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning a certain monk at Sāvatthī. Venerable sir, a certain monk invited another monk, who had eaten and been invited to stop, to partake of staple food that was not a leftover. Venerable sir, it was laid down in that incident.

4. Bhojanavagga, 7. vikālabhojana sikkhāpucchā

4. The Chapter on Food, 7. Question on the Training Rule concerning Untimely Food

Pucchā – vikāle āvuso khādanīyaṃ vā bhojanīyaṃ vā bhuñjantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The Question – Friend, where was the pācittiya offense laid down for eating chewable food or staple food at the wrong time? Concerning whom? In what incident was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – rājagahe bhante sattarasavaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, sattarasavaggiyā bhante bhikkhū vikāle bhojanaṃ bhuñjiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The Reply – Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the monks of the Group of Seventeen at Rājagaha. Venerable sir, the monks of the Group of Seventeen partook of food at the wrong time. Venerable sir, it was laid down in that incident.

4. Bhojanavagga, 8. sannidhikāraka sikkhāpucchā

4. The Chapter on Food, 8. Question on the Training Rule concerning Storing Food

Pucchā – sannidhikārakaṃ āvuso khādanīyaṃ vā bhojanīyaṃ vā bhuñjantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The Question – Friend, where was the pācittiya offense laid down for eating stored-up chewable food or staple food? Concerning whom? In what incident was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante āyasmantaṃ belaṭṭhasīsaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante belaṭṭhasīso sannidhikārakaṃ bhojanaṃ bhuñji, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The Reply – Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning Venerable Belaṭṭhasīsa at Sāvatthī. Venerable sir, Venerable Belaṭṭhasīsa partook of stored-up food. Venerable sir, it was laid down in that incident.

4. Bhojanavagga, 9. paṇītabhojana sikkhāpucchā

4. The Chapter on Food, 9. Question on the Training Rule concerning Fine Foods

Pucchā – paṇīta bhojanāni āvuso attano atthāya viññāpetvā bhuñjantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The Question – Friend, where was the pācittiya offense laid down for one who, having requested fine foods for oneself, then eats them? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what incident?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ [M 1.0163] bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū paṇītabhojanāni attano atthāya viññāpetvā bhuñjiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The Reply – Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the group-of-six monks at Sāvatthī. Venerable sir, the group-of-six monks, having requested fine foods for their own benefit, ate them. Venerable sir, it was laid down in that incident.

4. Bhojanavagga, 10. dantapona sikkhāpucchā

4. The Chapter on Food, 10. Question on the Training Rule concerning a Tooth-pick

Pucchā – adinnaṃ āvuso mukhadvāraṃ āhāraṃ āharantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The Question – Friend, where was the pācittiya offense laid down for taking food into the mouth that has not been given? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what incident?

Vissajjanā – vesāliyaṃ bhante aññataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, aññataro bhante bhikkhu adinnaṃ mukhadvāraṃ āhāraṃ āhari, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The Reply – Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning a certain monk at Vesālī. Venerable sir, a certain monk took food into his mouth that had not been given. Venerable sir, it was laid down in that incident.

5. Acelakavagga, 1. acelaka sikkhāpucchā

5. The Chapter on Naked Ascetics, 1. Question on the Training Rule concerning a Naked Ascetic

Pucchā – acelakassavā [M 1.0164] āvuso paribbājakassavā paribbājikāyavā sahatthā khādanīyaṃvā bhojanīyaṃvā dentassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The Question – Friend, where was the pācittiya offense laid down for one who gives, with one’s own hand, chewable food or staple food to a naked ascetic, a male wanderer, or a female wanderer? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what incident?

Vissajjanā – vesāliyaṃ bhante āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante ānando aññatarissā paribbājikāya ekaṃ maññamāno dve pūve adāsi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The Reply – Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning Venerable Ānanda at Vesālī. Venerable sir, Venerable Ānanda, thinking it was one, gave two cakes to a certain female wanderer. Venerable sir, it was laid down in that incident.

5. Acelakavagga, 2. uyyojana sikkhāpucchā

5. The Chapter on Naked Ascetics, 2. Question on the Training Rule concerning Dismissal

Pucchā – bhikkhuṃ [M 1.0165] ehāvuso gāmaṃvā nigamaṃvā piṇḍāya pavisissāmāti tassa dāpetvāvā adāpetvāvā uyyojentassa āvuso pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.
