The Question – Friend, where was the pācittiya offense laid down for one who, after saying to a monk, “Come, friend, let us enter the village or town for alms,” dismisses him, whether having given him something or not? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what incident?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante upanando sakyaputto bhikkhu ehāvuso gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pavisissāmāti tassa adāpetvā uyyojesi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The Reply – Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning Venerable Upananda the Sakyan. Venerable sir, Venerable Upananda the Sakyan, after saying to a monk, “Come, friend, let us enter the village for alms,” dismissed him without having given him anything. Venerable sir, it was laid down in that incident.

5. Acelakavagga, 3. sabhojana sikkhāpucchā

5. The Chapter on Naked Ascetics, 3. Question on the Training Rule concerning a Family that is Eating

Pucchā – sabhojane kule anupakhajja nisajjaṃ kappentassa āvuso pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The Question – Friend, where was the pācittiya offense laid down for one who makes a practice of sitting down intrusively in a family that is eating? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what incident?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ [M 1.0166] bhante āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante upanando sakyaputto sabhojane kule anupakhajja nisajjaṃ kappesi, tasmiṃ bhantevatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The Reply – Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning Venerable Upananda the Sakyan. Venerable sir, Venerable Upananda the Sakyan made a practice of sitting down intrusively in a family that was eating. Venerable sir, it was laid down in that incident.

5. Acelakavagga 4-5. rahopaṭicchanna, rahonisajja sikkhāpucchā

5. The Chapter on Naked Ascetics, 4-5. Questions on the Training Rules concerning a Private, Concealed Place, and Sitting Alone in Private

Pucchā – mātugāmena saddhiṃ raho paṭicchanne āsane nisajjaṃ kappentassaca āvuso mātugāmena saddhiṃ eko ekāya raho nisajjaṃ kappentassaca pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The Question – Friend, where was the pācittiya offense laid down for one who makes a practice of sitting with a woman on a private, concealed seat, and for one who makes a practice of sitting alone with a woman in a private place? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what incident?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante upanando sakyaputto mātugāmena saddhiṃ raho paṭicchanne āsane nisajjaṃ kappesi, mātugāmena saddhiṃ eko ekāya raho nisajjaṃ kappesi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

The Reply – Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning Venerable Upananda the Sakyan. Venerable sir, Venerable Upananda the Sakyan made a practice of sitting with a woman on a private, concealed seat, and he made a practice of sitting alone with a woman in a private place. Venerable sir, it was laid down in that incident.

5. Acelakavagga, 6. cāritta sikkhāpucchā

5. The Chapter on Naked Ascetics, 6. Question on the Training Rule concerning Frequenting Families

Pucchā – nimantitena [M 1.0167] āvuso sabhattena santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā purebhattaṃvā pacchābhattaṃvā kulesu cārittaṃ āpajjantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense of expiation laid down for one who, having been invited to a communal meal, frequents families before or after the meal without asking a monk who is present? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what connection?

Vissajjanā – rājagahe bhante āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante upanando sakyaputto nimantito sabhatto samāno purebhattaṃ pacchābhattaṃ kulesu cārittaṃ āpajji, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Rājagaha concerning the venerable Upananda the Sakyan. Venerable sir, the venerable Upananda the Sakyan, having been invited to a communal meal, frequented families before and after the meal. It was laid down in that connection, venerable sir.

5. Acelakavagga, 7. mahānāma sikkhāpucchā

5. The Chapter on the Naked Ascetics, 7. Mahānāma's Training Question

Pucchā – tatuttari āvuso bhesajjaṃ viññāpentassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense of expiation laid down for one who requests medicine beyond what is allowable? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what connection?

Vissajjanā – sakkesu [M 1.0168] bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū mahānāmena sakkena ajjaṇho bhante āgametāti vuccamānā nāgamesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down among the Sakyans concerning the group-of-six monks. Venerable sir, the group-of-six monks, when told by Mahānāma the Sakyan, “Venerable sirs, please come tomorrow,” did not come. It was laid down in that connection, venerable sir.

5. Acelakavagga 8-9-10. uyyuttasenā caso sikkhāpucchā

5. The Chapter on the Naked Ascetics, 8-9-10. Questions concerning the Arrayed Army

Pucchā – uyyuttaṃ āvuso senaṃ dassanāya gacchantassa ca atirekatirattaṃ senāya vasantassa ca uyyodhikaṃ gacchantassa ca pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense of expiation laid down for one who goes to see an arrayed army, for one who stays with the army for more than three nights, and for one who goes to a battle formation? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what connection?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū uyyuttaṃ senaṃ dassanāya agamaṃsu, teyeva bhante chabbaggiyā bhikkhū atirekatirattaṃ senāya vasiṃsu, teyeva bhante chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uyyodhikaṃ agamaṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the group-of-six monks. Venerable sir, the group-of-six monks went to see an arrayed army. Venerable sir, those same group-of-six monks stayed with the army for more than three nights. Venerable sir, those same group-of-six monks went to a battle formation. It was laid down in that connection, venerable sir.

6. Surāpānavagga, 1. surāpāna sikkhāpucchā

6. The Chapter on Intoxicants, 1. Question concerning Drinking Intoxicants

Pucchā – surāmerayapāne [M 1.0169] āvuso pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense of expiation laid down for drinking liquor and fermented liquor? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what connection?

Vissajjanā – kosambiyaṃ bhante āyasmantaṃ sāgataṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante sāgato majjaṃ pivi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Kosambī concerning the venerable Sāgata. Venerable sir, the venerable Sāgata drank an intoxicant. It was laid down in that connection, venerable sir.

6. Surāpānavagga, 2. aṅgulipatodaka sikkhāpucchā

6. The Chapter on Intoxicants, 2. Question concerning Poking with a Finger

Pucchā – aṅgulipatodake [M 1.0170] āvuso pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense of expiation laid down for poking with a finger? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what connection?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ aṅgulipatodakena hāsesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the group-of-six monks. Venerable sir, the group-of-six monks made a monk laugh by poking him with a finger. It was laid down in that connection, venerable sir.

6. Surāpānavagga, 3. hasadhamma sikkhāpucchā

6. The Chapter on Intoxicants, 3. Question on the Training Rule Concerning Playful Behavior

Pucchā – udake hasadhamme āvuso pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense of expiation for playful behavior in water laid down? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what connection?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante sattarasavaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, sattarasavaggiyā bhante bhikkhū aciravatiyā nadiyā udake kīḷiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the group-of-seventeen monks. Venerable sir, the group-of-seventeen monks were playing in the water of the Aciravatī River. It was laid down in that connection, venerable sir.

6. Surāpānavagga, 4. anādariya sikkhāpucchā

6. The Chapter on Intoxicants, 4. Question on the Training Rule Concerning Disrespect

Pucchā – anādariya [M 1.0171] āvuso pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense of expiation for disrespect laid down? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what connection?

Vissajjanā – kosambiyaṃ bhante āyasmantaṃ channaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante channo āpattiyā anuyuñjiyamāno anādariyaṃ akāsi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Kosambī concerning the Venerable Channa. Venerable sir, the Venerable Channa, when questioned about an offense, showed disrespect. It was laid down in that connection, venerable sir.

6. Surāpānavagga, 5. bhiṃsāpana sikkhāpucchā

6. The Chapter on Intoxicants, 5. Question on the Training Rule Concerning Frightening

Pucchā – bhikkhuṃ [M 1.0172] bhiṃsāpentassa āvuso pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense of expiation for frightening a monk laid down? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what connection?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ bhiṃsāpesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the group-of-six monks. Venerable sir, the group-of-six monks frightened a monk. It was laid down in that connection, venerable sir.

6. Surāpānavagga, 6. jotika sikkhāpucchā

6. The Chapter on Intoxicants, 6. Question on the Training Rule Concerning Kindling a Fire

Pucchā – jotiṃ āvuso samādahitvā visibbentassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense of expiation laid down for one who, having kindled a fire, extinguishes it? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what connection?

Vissajjanā – bhaggesu bhante sambahule bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhū jotiṃ samādapetvā visibbesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down in Bhagga concerning many monks. Venerable sir, many monks, having had a fire kindled, extinguished it. It was laid down in that connection, venerable sir.

6. Surāpānavagga, 7. nahāna sikkhāpucchā

6. The Chapter on Intoxicants, 7. Question on the Training Rule Concerning Bathing

Pucchā – orenaddhamāsaṃ [M 1.0173] āvuso nahāyantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense of expiation for one who bathes more often than once a fortnight laid down? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what connection?

Vissajjanā – rājagahe bhante sambahule bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhū rājānampi passitvā na mattaṃ jānitvā nahāyiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Rājagaha concerning many monks. Venerable sir, many monks, even after seeing the king, did not know moderation and bathed. It was laid down in that connection, venerable sir.

Pucchā – kassa [M 1.0174] āvuso vacanaṃ.

Question: Whose word is this, friend?

Vissajjanā – bhagavato bhante vacanaṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa.

Reply: “Venerable sir, this is the word of the Blessed One, the Arahant, the Perfectly Enlightened One.”

Pucchā – kenāvuso ābhataṃ.

Question: “Friend, by whom was this brought?”

Vissajjanā – paramparāya bhante ābhataṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it has been brought down by tradition.”

Pucchā – atthi nukho āvuso ettha kocipi viruddhadoso.

Question: “Friend, is there any fault or contradiction here?”

Vissajjanā – natthi bhante.

Reply: “Venerable sir, there is none.”

Suṇātu me bhante saṅgho…pe… saṃgāyeyyāma.

“Venerable sirs, let the Saṅgha hear me… let us recite.”

Pucchā – yaṃ [M 1.0175] tena āvuso…pe… sammāsambuddhena anādiyitvā tiṇṇaṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇānaṃ aññataraṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇaṃ navaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñjantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the pācittiya offense laid down by that… Perfectly Enlightened One for one who uses a new robe without having applied one of the three means of discoloration? Concerning whom was it laid down? In what case was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante sambahule bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhū attano cīvaraṃ na sañjāniṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning many monks. Venerable sir, many monks did not recognize their own robes. Venerable sir, it was laid down in that case.”

6. Surāpānavagga, 10. cīvaraapanidhāna sikkhāpucchā

6. The Chapter on Drinking Liquor, 10. Question on the Training Rule Concerning Hiding a Robe

Pucchā – bhikkhussa āvuso pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā nisīdanaṃ vā sūcigharaṃ vā kāyabandhanaṃ vā apanidhentassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the pācittiya offense laid down for one who hides a monk’s bowl, robe, sitting cloth, needle-case, or waistband? Concerning whom was it laid down? In what case was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ [M 1.0176] bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū bhikkhūnaṃ pattampi cīvarampi apanidhesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the group-of-six monks. Venerable sir, the group-of-six monks hid the bowls and robes of monks. Venerable sir, it was laid down in that case.”

7. Sappāṇakavagga, 1. sañcicca sikkhāpucchā

7. The Chapter on Living Beings, 1. Question on the Training Rule Concerning Intention

Pucchā – sañcicca āvuso pāṇaṃ jīvitā voropentassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the pācittiya offense laid down for one who intentionally deprives a living being of life? Concerning whom was it laid down? In what case was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ āyasmā bhante udāyī sañcicca pāṇaṃ jīvitā voropesi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the Venerable Udāyī. Venerable sir, the Venerable Udāyī intentionally deprived a living being of life. Venerable sir, it was laid down in that case.”

7. Sappāṇakavagga, 2. sappāṇaka sikkhāpucchā

7. The Chapter on Living Beings, 2. Question on the Training Rule Concerning Water with Living Beings

Pucchā – jānaṃ [M 1.0177] āvuso sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ paribhuñjantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the pācittiya offense laid down for one who knowingly uses water containing living beings? Concerning whom was it laid down? In what case was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ paribhuñjiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the group-of-six monks. Venerable sir, the group-of-six monks knowingly used water containing living beings. Venerable sir, it was laid down in that case.”

7. Sappāṇakavagga, 6. theyyasattha sikkhāpucchā

7. The Chapter on Living Beings, 6. Question on the Training Rule Concerning a Caravan of Thieves

Pucchā – jānaṃ āvuso theyyasatthena saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya ekaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipajjantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the pācittiya offense laid down for one who, knowingly making an arrangement with a caravan of thieves, travels along the same road? Concerning whom was it laid down? In what case was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante aññataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, aññataro bhante bhikkhu jānaṃ theyyasatthena saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya ekaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipajji, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning a certain monk. Venerable sir, a certain monk, knowingly making an arrangement with a caravan of thieves, traveled along the same road. Venerable sir, it was laid down in that case.”

7. Sappāṇakavagga, 7. saṃvidhāna sikkhāpucchā

7. The Chapter on Living Beings, 7. Question on the Training Rule Concerning an Arrangement

Pucchā – mātugāmena [M 1.0178] saddhiṃ āvuso saṃvidhāya ekaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipajjantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the pācittiya offense laid down for one who, having made an arrangement with a woman, travels along the same road? Concerning whom was it laid down? In what case was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante aññataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, aññataro bhante bhikkhu mātugāmena saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya ekaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipajji, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning a certain monk. Venerable sir, a certain monk, having made an arrangement with a woman, traveled along the same road. Venerable sir, it was laid down in that case.”

8. Sahadhammikavagga, 1. sahadhammika sikkhāpucchā

8. The Chapter on Righteous Matters, 1. Question on the Training Rule Concerning Righteous Admonition

Pucchā – bhikkhūhi āvuso sahadhammikaṃ vuccamānena na tāvāhaṃ āvuso etasmiṃ sikkhāpade sikkhissāmi, yāva na aññaṃ bhikkhuṃ byattaṃ vinayadharaṃ paripucchissāmīti bhaṇantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the pācittiya offense laid down for one who, when being spoken to righteously by monks, says, ‘Friends, I will not train in this training rule until I have asked another monk who is skilled and a Vinaya-holder’? Concerning whom was it laid down? In what case was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – kosambiyaṃ [M 1.0179] bhante āyasmantaṃ channaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante channo bhikkhūhi sahadhammikaṃ vuccamāno na tāvāhaṃ āvuso etasmiṃ sikkhāpade sikkhissāmi, yāva na aññaṃ bhikkhuṃ byattaṃ vinayadharaṃ paripucchissāmīti bhaṇi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down at Kosambī concerning the Venerable Channa. Venerable sir, when the Venerable Channa was being spoken to righteously by the monks, he said: ‘Friends, I will not train in this training rule until I have asked another monk who is skilled and a Vinaya-holder.’ Venerable sir, it was laid down in that case.”

8. Sahadhammikavagga, 2. vilekhana sikkhāpucchā

8. The Chapter on Righteous Matters, 2. Question on the Training Rule Concerning Disparaging

Pucchā – vinayaṃ vivaṇṇentassa āvuso pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the pācittiya offense laid down for one who disparages the Vinaya? Concerning whom was it laid down? In what case was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū vinayaṃ vivaṇṇesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the group-of-six monks. Venerable sir, the group-of-six monks disparaged the Vinaya. Venerable sir, it was laid down in that case.”

8. Sahadhammikavagga, 3. mohana sikkhāpucchā

8. The Chapter on Righteous Matters, 3. Question on the Training Rule Concerning Causing Confusion

Pucchā – mohanake [M 1.0180] āvuso pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the pācittiya rule regarding causing delusion established? Concerning whom was it established, and in what instance?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū mohesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was established at Sāvatthī concerning the group of six monks. Venerable sir, the group of six monks caused delusion. It was established in that instance, venerable sir.

Pucchā – bhikkhussa [M 1.0181] āvuso kupitena anattamanena pahāraṃ dentassa ca talasattikaṃ uggirantassa ca pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the pācittiya rule established for a monk who, being angry and displeased, gives a blow and raises his hand threateningly? Concerning whom was it established, and in what instance?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū kupitā anattamanā sattarasavaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pahāraṃ adaṃsu, talasattikañca uggiriṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was established at Sāvatthī concerning the group of six monks. Venerable sir, the group of six monks, being angry and displeased, gave a blow to the group of seventeen monks and raised their hands threateningly. It was established in that instance, venerable sir.

8. Sahadhammikavagga, 7. sañcicca sikkhāpucchā

8. The Chapter on Fellow Practitioners, 7. The Training Question on Intentionally Causing Remorse

Pucchā – bhikkhussa āvuso sañcicca kukkuccaṃ upadahantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, for a monk who intentionally causes remorse, where was the pācittiya rule established? Concerning whom was it established, and in what instance?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū bhikkhūnaṃ sañcicca kukkuccaṃ upadahiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was established at Sāvatthī concerning the group of six monks. Venerable sir, the group of six monks intentionally caused remorse for other monks. It was established in that instance, venerable sir.

8. Sahadhammikavagga, 8. upassuti sikkhāpucchā

8. The Chapter on Fellow Practitioners, 8. The Training Question on Eavesdropping

Pucchā – bhikkhūnaṃ [M 1.0182] āvuso bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādā pannānaṃ upassutiṃ tiṭṭhantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, for one who stands eavesdropping on monks who have become quarrelsome, contentious, and have fallen into dispute, where was the pācittiya rule established? Concerning whom was it established, and in what instance?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū bhikkhūnaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalaha jātānaṃ vivādāpannānaṃ upassutiṃ tiṭṭhahiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was established at Sāvatthī concerning the group of six monks. Venerable sir, the group of six monks stood eavesdropping on monks who had become quarrelsome, contentious, and had fallen into dispute. It was established in that instance, venerable sir.

8. Sahadhammikavagga, 9. kammapaṭibāhana sikkhāpucchā

8. The Chapter on Fellow Practitioners, 9. The Training Question on Objecting to a Formal Act

Pucchā – dhammikānaṃ āvuso kammānaṃ chandaṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpajjantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, for one who, having given consent for lawful formal acts, afterwards complains, where was the pācittiya rule established? Concerning whom was it established, and in what instance?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ [M 1.0183] bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū dhammikānaṃ kammānaṃ chandaṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpajjiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was established at Sāvatthī concerning the group of six monks. Venerable sir, the group of six monks, having given their consent for lawful formal acts, afterwards complained. It was established in that instance, venerable sir.

8. Sahadhammikavagga 10. chandaṃ adatvāgamana sikkhāpucchā

8. The Chapter on Fellow Practitioners, 10. The Training Question on Departing Without Giving Consent

Pucchā – saṅghe āvuso vinicchakathāya vattamānāya chandaṃ adatvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkamantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, for one who, while a discussion for adjudication is proceeding in the Sangha, rises from his seat and departs without giving consent, where was the pācittiya rule established? Concerning whom was it established, and in what instance?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante aññataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, aññataro bhante chabbaggiyo bhikkhu saṅghe vinicchayakathāya vattamānāya chandaṃ adatvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pakkāmi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was established at Sāvatthī concerning a certain monk. Venerable sir, a certain monk from the group of six, while a discussion for adjudication was proceeding in the Sangha, rose from his seat and departed without giving his consent. It was established in that instance, venerable sir.

8. Sahadhammikavagga, 12. pariṇāmana sikkhāpucchā

8. The Chapter on Fellow Practitioners, 12. The Training Question on Diversion

Pucchā – jānaṃ [M 1.0184] āvuso saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ puggalassa pariṇāmentassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, for one who knowingly diverts to an individual a gain that had been dedicated to the Sangha, where was the pācittiya rule established? Concerning whom was it established, and in what instance?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ puggalassa pariṇāmesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was established at Sāvatthī concerning the group of six monks. Venerable sir, the group of six monks knowingly diverted to an individual a gain that had been dedicated to the Sangha. It was established in that instance, venerable sir.

9. Ratanavagga, 1. antepura sikkhāpucchā

9. The Chapter on Jewels, 1. The Training Question on the Inner Palace

Pucchā – pubbe appaṭisaṃviditena āvuso rañño antepuraṃ pavisantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, for one entering the king's inner palace without having been announced beforehand, where was the pācittiya rule established? Concerning whom was it established, and in what instance?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ [M 1.0185] bhante āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, āyasmā bhante ānando pubbe appaṭisaṃvidito rañño antepuraṃ pāvisi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was established at Sāvatthī concerning the Venerable Ānanda. Venerable sir, the Venerable Ānanda entered the king’s inner palace without having been announced beforehand. It was established in that instance, venerable sir.

9. Ratanavagga, 2. ratana sikkhāpucchā

9. The Chapter on Jewels, 2. The Training Question on a Jewel

Pucchā – ratanaṃ āvuso uggaṇhantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, for one who picks up a jewel, where was the pācittiya rule established? Concerning whom was it established, and in what instance?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante aññataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, aññataro bhante bhikkhu ratanaṃ uggahesi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was established at Sāvatthī concerning a certain monk. Venerable sir, a certain monk picked up a jewel. It was established in that instance, venerable sir.

9. Ratanavagga, 3. vikālagāmappavisana sikkhāpucchā

9. The Chapter on Jewels, 3. The Training Question on Entering a Village at an Improper Time

Pucchā – santaṃ [M 1.0186] āvuso bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā vikāle gāmaṃ pavisantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, for one who enters a village at an improper time without asking a present monk, where was the pācittiya rule established? Concerning whom was it established, and in what instance?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā vikāle gāmaṃ pavisiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was established at Sāvatthī concerning the group of six monks. Venerable sir, the group of six monks entered a village at an improper time without asking a present monk. It was established in that instance, venerable sir.

Pāṭidesanīya

Offenses to be Confessed

3. Tatiyapāṭidesanī sikkhāpucchā

3. The Training Question on the Third Offense to be Confessed

Pucchā – sekkhasammatesu [M 1.0187] āvuso kulesu khādanīyaṃ vā bhojanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahetvā bhuñjantassa pāṭidesanīyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the Pāṭidesanīya offense established for one who, in families designated as trainees, receives with his own hand and eats chewable food or edible food? Concerning whom, and in what matter was it established?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante sambahule bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhū namattaṃ jānitvā paṭiggahesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was established at Sāvatthī concerning several monks. Venerable sir, several monks, knowing she was not a relative, accepted it. Venerable sir, it was established concerning that matter.

Sekhiyakaṇḍa

The Sekhiya Section

Pucchā – anādariyaṃ [M 1.0188] āvuso paṭicca purato vā pacchato vā olambentena nivāsentassaca pārupantassaca dukkaṭaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the dukkaṭa offense established for one who, due to disrespect, lets his lower robe hang down in front or behind while putting on his lower robe and outer robe? Concerning whom, and in what matter was it established?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū purato ca pacchato ca olambantā nivāsesuṃceva pārupiṃsuca, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was established at Sāvatthī concerning the group of six monks. Venerable sir, the group of six monks, when putting on their lower robes and outer robes, let them hang down in front and behind. Venerable sir, it was established concerning that matter.

Pucchā – anādariyaṃ āvuso paṭicca kāyaṃ vivaritvā antaraghare gacchantassa ca nisīdantassa ca dukkaṭaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the dukkaṭa offense established for one who, due to disrespect, having uncovered his body, walks or sits inside an inhabited area? Concerning whom, and in what matter was it established?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggīye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū kāyaṃ vivaritvā antaraghare gacchiṃsu ceva nisīdiṃsuca, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was established at Sāvatthī concerning the group of six monks. Venerable sir, the group of six monks, having uncovered their bodies, went and sat down inside an inhabited area. Venerable sir, it was established concerning that matter.

Pucchā – anādariyaṃ [M 1.0189] āvuso paṭicca hatthaṃ vā pādaṃ vā kīḷāpentena antaraghare gacchantassa ca nisīdantassa ca, tahaṃ tahaṃ olokentena antaraghare gacchantassa ca nisīdantassa ca, ukkhittakāya antaraghare gacchantassa ca nisīdantassa ca dukkaṭaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the dukkaṭa offense established for one who, due to disrespect, swings his hands or feet while walking or sitting inside an inhabited area; or for one who, looking here and there, walks or sits inside an inhabited area; or for one who, with body swaying, walks or sits inside an inhabited area? Concerning whom, and in what matter was it established?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū hatthampi pādampi kīḷāpentā tahaṃ tahaṃpi olokentā antaraghare gacchiṃsuceva nisīdiṃsu ca, teyeva bhante chabbaggiyā bhikkhū ukkhittakāya antaraghare gacchiṃsuceva nisīdiṃsuca, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was established at Sāvatthī concerning the group of six monks. Venerable sir, the group of six monks, swinging their hands and feet and looking here and there, both went and sat down inside an inhabited area. Venerable sir, those same group of six monks, with their bodies swaying, both went and sat down inside an inhabited area. Venerable sir, it was established concerning that matter.

Pucchā – anādariyaṃ [M 1.0190] āvuso paṭicca ujjagghikāya vā uccāsaddaṃ mahāsaddaṃ karontena vā antaraghare gacchantassaca nisīdantassaca dukkaṭaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the dukkaṭa offense established for one who, due to disrespect, laughs loudly or makes a loud, great noise while walking or sitting inside an inhabited area? Concerning whom, and in what matter was it established?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū mahāhasitaṃ hasantā ca uccāsaddaṃ mahāsaddaṃ karontā ca antaraghare gacchiṃsuceva nisīdiṃsuca, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was established at Sāvatthī concerning the group of six monks. Venerable sir, the group of six monks, laughing loudly and making a loud, great noise, went and sat down inside an inhabited area. Venerable sir, it was established concerning that matter.

Pucchā – anādariyaṃ āvuso paṭicca kāyappacālakaṃ vā bāhuppa cālakaṃ vā sīsappacālakaṃ vā khambhakatena vā oguṇṭhitena vā antaraghare gacchantassa ca nisīdantassa ca ukkuṭikāya kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the dukkaṭa offense established for one who, due to disrespect, sways the body, or swings the arms, or shakes the head, or walks with hands on hips, or with head covered, or squats, while walking or sitting inside an inhabited area? Concerning whom, and in what matter was it established?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū kāyappacālakaṃ bāhuppacālakaṃ sīsappacālakaṃ karontā ca khambhīkatā ca oguṇṭhitā ca antaraghare gacchiṃsuceva nisīdiṃsuca, ukkuṭikāyaca antaraghare gacchiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was established at Sāvatthī concerning the group of six monks. Venerable sir, the group of six monks, swaying their bodies, swinging their arms, and shaking their heads, and with hands on hips and with head covered, both went and sat down inside an inhabited area. They also went inside an inhabited area squatting. Venerable sir, it was established concerning that matter.

Pucchā – anādariyaṃ [M 1.0191] āvuso paṭicca pallatthikāya antaraghare nisinnassa dukkaṭaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the dukkaṭa offense established for one who, due to disrespect, sits cross-legged inside an inhabited area? Concerning whom, and in what matter was it established?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū pallatthikāya antaraghare nisīdiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was established at Sāvatthī concerning the group of six monks. Venerable sir, the group of six monks sat cross-legged inside an inhabited area. Venerable sir, it was established concerning that matter.

Pucchā – anādariyaṃ [M 1.0192] āvuso paṭicca asakkaccaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggaṇhantassa ca tahaṃ tahaṃ olokentena piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggaṇhantassaca sūpaññeva bahuṃ paṭiggaṇhantassaca thūpīkataṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggaṇhantassaca dukkaṭaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the dukkaṭa offense established for one who, due to disrespect, receives almsfood disrespectfully; and for one who receives almsfood looking here and there; and for one who receives mostly soup; and for one who receives almsfood heaped up? Concerning whom, and in what matter was it established?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū asakkaccaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggahesuṃ, tahaṃ tahaṃ olokentā piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggahesuṃ, sūpaññeva bahuṃ paṭiggahesuṃ, thūpīkataṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggahesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was established at Sāvatthī concerning the group of six monks. Venerable sir, the group of six monks received almsfood disrespectfully. They received almsfood looking here and there. They received mostly soup. They received almsfood heaped up. Venerable sir, it was established concerning that matter.

Pucchā – anādariyaṃ [M 1.0193] āvuso paṭicca āsakkaccaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñjantassaca tahaṃ tahaṃ olokentana piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñjantassa ca tahaṃ tahaṃ omasitvā piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñjantassa ca sūpaññeva bahuṃ bhuñjantassa ca thūpakato omadditvā piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñjantassa ca sūpaṃ vā byañjanaṃ vā odanena paṭicchādentassa ca dukkaṭaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense of wrongdoing laid down for one who, out of disrespect, eats almsfood without reverence; or who eats almsfood while looking around here and there; or who eats almsfood while touching it here and there; or who eats much soup; or who eats almsfood after pressing it down from a heap; or who covers soup or relish with rice? Concerning whom, in what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū asakkaccaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñjiṃsu, tahaṃ tahaṃ olokentā piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñjiṃsu, teyeva bhante chabbaggiyā bhikkhū tahaṃ tahaṃ omasitvā piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñjiṃsu, teyeva bhante chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bahuṃ sūpaṃ bhuñjiṃsu, teyeva bhante chabbaggiyā bhikkhū thūpakato omadditvā piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñjiṃsu, teyeva bhante chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sūpampi byañjanampi odanena paṭicchādesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the monks of the group of six. The monks of the group of six, venerable sir, ate almsfood disrespectfully. They ate almsfood while looking around here and there. Those very monks of the group of six, venerable sir, ate almsfood while touching it here and there. Those very monks of the group of six, venerable sir, ate much soup. Those very monks of the group of six, venerable sir, ate almsfood after pressing it down from a heap. Those very monks of the group of six, venerable sir, covered both soup and relish with rice. Concerning that matter, venerable sir, it was laid down.

Pucchā – anādariyaṃ [M 1.0194] āvuso paṭicca sūpaṃvā odanaṃvā agilānena attano atthāya viññāpetvā bhuñjantassa dukkaṭaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, out of disrespect, for one who, not being ill, requests and eats soup or rice for his own sake, where was the offense of wrongdoing laid down? Concerning whom? On what matter was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū sūpampi odanampi attano atthāya viññāpetvā bhuñjiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the monks of the group of six. The monks of the group of six, venerable sir, requested and ate both soup and rice for their own sake. Concerning that matter, venerable sir, it was laid down.

Pucchā – anādariyaṃ āvuso paṭicca ujjhānasaññinā paresaṃ pattaṃ olokentassa ca atimahantaṃ kabaḷaṃ karontassa ca dīghaṃ ālopaṃ karontassa ca dukkaṭaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, out of disrespect, for one who is critical, looking at others' bowls; or who makes too large a mouthful; or who makes a long morsel—where was the offense of wrong conduct established? Concerning whom? On what matter was it established?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ [M 1.0195] bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū ujjhānasaññino paresaṃ pattaṃ olokesuṃ, teyeva bhante chabbaggiyā bhikkhū atimahantaṃ kabaḷaṃ kariṃsu, teyeva bhante chabbaggiyā bhikkhū dīghaṃ ālopaṃ kariṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Answer: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the monks of the group of six. The monks of the group of six, venerable sir, being critical, looked at others’ bowls. Those same monks of the group of six, venerable sir, made too large a mouthful. Those same monks of the group of six, venerable sir, made a long morsel. On that matter, venerable sir, it was laid down.

Pucchā – anādariyaṃ āvuso paṭicca anāhaṭe kabaḷe mukhadvāraṃ vivarantassa ca bhuñjamāne sabbaṃ hatthaṃ mukhe pakkhipantassa ca sakabaḷena mukhena byāharantassa ca piṇḍukkhepakaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñjantassa ca kabaḷāvacchedakaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñjantassa ca avagaṇḍakārakaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñjantassa ca hatthaniddhunakaṃ sitthāvakārakaṃ jivhānicchārakaṃ capucapukārakaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñjantassa ca dukkaṭaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, out of disrespect, for one who opens his mouth before the morsel is brought to it; and who, while eating, puts his whole hand into his mouth; and who speaks with his mouth full; and who eats almsfood by tossing up lumps; and who eats almsfood by cutting off morsels; and who eats almsfood by stuffing his cheeks; and who eats almsfood while shaking his hand, scattering rice, sticking out his tongue, or making a smacking sound—where was the offense of wrongdoing laid down? Concerning whom, and on what matter, was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū anāhaṭe kabaḷe mukhadvāraṃ vivariṃsu, bhuñjamānā sabbaṃ hatthaṃ mukhe pakkhipiṃsu, sakabaḷena mukhena byāhariṃsu, piṇḍukkhepakaṃ bhuñjiṃsu, kabaḷāvacchedakaṃ bhuñjiṃsu, avagaṇḍakārakaṃ bhuñjiṃsu, hatthaniddhunakaṃ sitthāvakārakaṃ jivhānicchārakaṃ [M 1.0196] capucapukārakaṃ bhuñjiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Answer: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the monks of the group of six. The monks of the group of six, venerable sir, opened their mouths before the morsel was brought to it; while eating, they put their whole hands into their mouths; they spoke with their mouths full; they ate by tossing up lumps; they ate by cutting off morsels; they ate by stuffing their cheeks; they ate while shaking their hands, scattering rice, sticking out their tongues, and making a smacking sound. On that matter, venerable sir, it was laid down.

Pucchā – anādariyaṃ āvuso paṭicca surusurukārakaṃ bhuñjantassaca hatthanillehakaṃvā pattanillehakaṃ vā oṭṭhanillehakaṃ vā bhuñjantassa ca sāmisena hatthena pānīyathālakaṃ paṭiggaṇhantassa ca sasitthakaṃ pattadhovanaṃ antaraghare caḍḍentassa ca dukkaṭaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, out of disrespect, for one who eats making a slurping sound; and who eats licking his hand, or licking the bowl, or licking his lips; and who receives a water vessel with a hand soiled with food; and who throws away bowl-washing water containing rice grains in an inhabited area—where was the offense of wrongdoing laid down? Concerning whom, and on what matter, was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – suru surukārakaṃ bhante bhuñjantassa dukkaṭaṃ kosambiyaṃ paññattaṃ, sambahule bhante bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhū surusurukārakaṃ khīraṃ piviṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ. Hatthanillehakaṃ [M 1.0197] bhuñjantassa ca pattanillehakaṃ bhuñjantassa ca oṭṭhanillehakaṃ bhuñjantassa dukkaṭaṃ sāvatthiyaṃ paññattaṃ, chabbaggiye bhante bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū hatthanillehakaṃ bhuñjiṃsu, pattanillehakaṃ bhuñjiṃsu, oṭṭhanillehakaṃ bhuñjiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Answer: For one who eats making a slurping sound, venerable sir, an offense of wrongdoing was laid down at Kosambī. It was laid down concerning a number of monks, venerable sir. A number of monks, venerable sir, drank milk while making a slurping sound. On that matter, venerable sir, it was laid down. For one who eats licking his hand, and who eats licking the bowl, and who eats licking his lips, an offense of wrongdoing was laid down at Sāvatthī. It was laid down concerning the monks of the group of six, venerable sir. The monks of the group of six, venerable sir, ate licking their hands, they ate licking the bowl, and they ate licking their lips. On that matter, venerable sir, it was laid down.

Sāmisena hatthena pānīyathālakaṃ paṭiggaṇhantassa ca sasitthakaṃ pattadhovanaṃ antaraghare chaḍḍentassa ca dukkaṭaṃ bhaggesu paññattaṃ, sambahule bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhikkhū sāmisena hatthena pānīyathālakaṃ paṭiggahesuṃ, teyeva bhante sambahulā bhikkhū sasitthakaṃ pattadhovanaṃ antaraghare chaḍḍesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

For one who receives a water vessel with a hand soiled with food, and for one who throws away bowl-washing water containing rice grains in an inhabited area, an offense of wrongdoing was laid down in the land of the Bhaggas. It was laid down concerning a number of monks. A number of monks received a water vessel with a hand soiled with food. Those same monks, venerable sir, threw away bowl-washing water containing rice grains in an inhabited area. On that matter, venerable sir, it was laid down.

Pucchā – anādariyaṃ [M 1.0198] āvuso paṭicca chattapāṇissa dhammaṃ desentassa ca daṇḍapāṇissa vā satthapāṇissa vā āvudhapāṇissa vā dhammaṃ desentassa ca dukkaṭaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, for one who, out of disrespect, teaches the Dhamma to a person holding an umbrella, and to a person holding a staff, or a knife, or a weapon—where was the offense of wrongdoing laid down? Concerning whom, and on what matter, was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū chattapāṇissa daṇḍapāṇissa satthapāṇissa āvudhapāṇissa dhammaṃ desesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Answer: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the monks of the group of six. The monks of the group of six, venerable sir, taught the Dhamma to a person holding an umbrella, a person holding a staff, a person holding a knife, and a person holding a weapon. On that matter, venerable sir, it was laid down.

Pucchā – anādariyaṃ āvuso paṭicca pādukāruḷhassa ca upahanāruḷhassa ca yānagatassa ca sayanagatassa ca dhammaṃ desentassa dukkaṭaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, for one who, out of disrespect, teaches the Dhamma to a person wearing sandals, or to a person wearing shoes, or to a person in a vehicle, or to a person lying down—where was the offense of wrongdoing laid down? Concerning whom, and on what matter, was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiye bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū pādukāruḷhassa upahanāruḷhassa yānagatassa sayanagatassa dhammaṃ desesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Answer: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the monks of the group of six. The monks of the group of six, venerable sir, taught the Dhamma to a person wearing sandals, a person wearing shoes, a person in a vehicle, and a person lying down. On that matter, venerable sir, it was laid down.

Pucchā – anādariyaṃ [M 1.0199] āvuso paṭicca pallatthikāya nisinnassa ca veṭhitasīsassa ca oguṇṭhitasīsassa ca chamāyaṃ nisīditvā āsane nisinnassa ca nīce āsane nisīditvā ucce āsane nisinnassa ca ṭhitena nisinnassa ca pacchato gacchantena purato gacchantassa ca uppathena gacchantena pathena gacchantassa ca dhammaṃ desentassa dukkaṭaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, for one who, out of disrespect, teaches the Dhamma to a person sitting with legs crossed, or with head wrapped, or with head covered; or who teaches while sitting on the ground to a person sitting on a seat; or while sitting on a low seat to a person sitting on a high seat; or while standing to a person who is sitting; or while walking behind a person walking in front; or while walking off the path to a person walking on the path—where was the offense of wrongdoing laid down? Concerning whom, and on what matter, was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiyeva bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū pallatthikāya nisinnassa ca veṭhitasīsassa ca oguṇṭhitasīsassa ca chamāyaṃ nisīditvā āsane nisinnassa ca nīce āsane nisīditvā ucce āsane nisinnassa ca ṭhitānisinnassa ca pacchato gacchantā purato gacchantassa ca uppathena gacchantā pathena gacchantassa ca dhammaṃ desesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Answer: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the monks of the group of six. The monks of the group of six, venerable sir, taught the Dhamma to a person sitting with legs crossed, or with head wrapped, or with head covered; and while sitting on the ground they taught a person sitting on a seat; and while sitting on a low seat they taught a person sitting on a high seat; and while standing they taught a person who was sitting; and while walking behind they taught a person walking in front; and while walking off the path they taught a person walking on the path. On that matter, venerable sir, it was laid down.

Pucchā – anādariyaṃ [M 1.0200] āvuso paṭicca ṭhitena uccāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā karontassa ca harite vā udake vā uccāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā kheḷaṃ vā karontassa ca dukkaṭaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, for one who, out of disrespect, defecates or urinates while standing; or who defecates, urinates, or spits on green grass or in water—where was the offense of wrongdoing laid down? Concerning whom, and on what matter, was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiyeva bhikkhū ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhū ṭhitā uccārampi passāvampi akaṃsu, teyeva bhante chabbaggiyā bhikkhū haritepi udakepi uccārampi passāvampi kheḷampi akaṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Answer: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the monks of the group of six. The monks of the group of six, venerable sir, defecated and urinated while standing. Those same monks of the group of six, venerable sir, defecated, urinated, and spat on green grass and in water. On that matter, venerable sir, it was laid down.

Bhikkhunīvibhaṅga

The Analysis of the Bhikkhunīs' Rules

5. Pañcama pārājika sikkhāpucchā

5. Question on the Fifth Pārājika Training Rule

Pucchā – bhikkhunīnaṃ [M 1.0202] āvuso pañcama pārājikaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the fifth Pārājika for bhikkhunīs laid down? Concerning whom, and on what matter, was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante sundarīnandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, sundarīnandā bhante bhikkhunī avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Answer: At Sāvatthī, venerable sir, it was laid down concerning the bhikkhunī Sundarīnandā. Venerable sir, the bhikkhunī Sundarīnandā, being filled with lust, consented to bodily contact with a man who was filled with lust. On that matter, venerable sir, it was laid down.

Bhikkhunīvibhaṅga, saṅghādisesa sikkhāpucchā

The Analysis of the Bhikkhunīs' Rules, Question on the Saṅghādisesa Training Rules

Pucchā – ussayavādikāya āvuso bhikkhuniyā aḍḍaṃ karontiyā saṅghādiseso kattha paññatto, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññatto.

Question: Friend, for a bhikkhunī given to contention who creates a dispute, where was the Saṅghādisesa laid down? Concerning whom, and on what matter, was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante tullanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha paññatto, thullanandā bhante bhikkhunī ussayavādikā vihari, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññatto.

Reply – At Sāvatthī, venerable sir, it was established concerning the bhikkhunī Thullanandā. Venerable sir, the bhikkhunī Thullanandā lived as one given to contention. In that circumstance, venerable sir, it was established.

Pucchā – coriṃ [M 1.0203] āvuso vuṭṭhāpentiyā saṅghādiseso kattha paññatto, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññatto.

Question – Friend, where was the Saṅghādisesa established concerning one who ordains a thief? Concerning whom and in what circumstance was it established?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante thullanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha paññatto, thullanandā bhante bhikkhunī coriṃ vuṭṭhāpesi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññatto.

Reply – At Sāvatthī, venerable sir, it was established concerning the bhikkhunī Thullanandā. Venerable sir, the bhikkhunī Thullanandā caused a thief to be ordained. In that circumstance, venerable sir, it was established.

Pucchā – ekāya [M 1.0204] āvuso gāmantaraṃ gacchantiyā saṅghādiseso kattha paññatto, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññatto.

Question – Friend, where was the Saṅghādisesa established concerning a bhikkhunī traveling alone to another village? Concerning whom and in what circumstance was it established?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante aññataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha paññatto, aññatarā bhante bhikkhunī ekā gāmantaraṃ gacchi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññatto.

Reply – At Sāvatthī, venerable sir, it was established concerning a certain bhikkhunī. Venerable sir, a certain bhikkhunī went alone to another village. In that circumstance, venerable sir, it was established.

Pucchā – avassutāya āvuso bhikkhuniyā avassutassa purisapuggalassa hatthato khādanīyaṃvā bhojanīyaṃvā sahatthā paṭiggahetvā bhuñjantassa saṅghādiseso kattha paññatto, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññatto.

Question – Friend, where was the Saṅghādisesa established for a bhikkhunī overcome by lust who, with her own hand, receives staple or non-staple food from the hand of a male person overcome by lust who is eating? Concerning whom and in what circumstance was it established?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante sundarīnandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha paññatto, sundarīnandā bhante bhikkhunī avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa [M 1.0205] hatthato āmisaṃ paṭiggahesi tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññatto.

Reply – At Sāvatthī, venerable sir, it was established concerning the bhikkhunī Sundarīnandā. Venerable sir, the bhikkhunī Sundarīnandā, being overcome by lust, received food from the hand of a male person overcome by lust. In that circumstance, venerable sir, it was established.

Pucchā – kupitāya āvuso anattamanāya bhikkhuniyā yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsantiyā na paṭinisajjantiyā saṅghādiseso kattha paññatto, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññatto.

Question – Friend, where was the Saṅghādisesa established for a bhikkhunī who, being angry and displeased, is admonished up to three times but does not desist? Concerning whom and in what circumstance was it established?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante caṇḍakāḷiṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha paññatto, caṇḍakāḷī bhante bhikkhunī kupitā anattamanā evaṃ abhaṇi, buddhaṃ paccācikkhāmi, dhammaṃ paccācikkhāmi, saṅghaṃ paccācikkhāmi, sikkhaṃ paccācikkhāmi, kinnumāva samaṇiyo yā samaṇiyo sakyadhītaro, santaññāpi samaṇiyo lajjiniyo kukkuccikā sikkhākāmā, tāsāhaṃ santike brahmacariyaṃ carissāmīti, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññatto.

Reply – At Sāvatthī, venerable sir, it was established concerning the bhikkhunī Caṇḍakālī. Venerable sir, the bhikkhunī Caṇḍakālī, being angry and displeased, spoke thus: "I renounce the Buddha, I renounce the Dhamma, I renounce the Saṅgha, I renounce the training. What are these ascetics, these daughters of the Sakyans? There are other ascetics who are conscientious, scrupulous, and desirous of training. I will live the holy life in their presence." In that circumstance, venerable sir, it was established.

Pucchā – kassa [M 1.0206] āvuso vacanaṃ.

Question – Friend, whose words are these?

Vissajjanā – bhagavato bhante vacanaṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa.

Reply – Venerable sir, this is the utterance of the Blessed One, the Arahant, the Perfectly Self-Enlightened One.

Pucchā – kenāvuso ābhataṃ.

Question – Friend, by whom was it brought?

Vissajjanā – paramparāya bhante ābhataṃ.

Reply – Venerable sir, it has been handed down through tradition.

Pucchā – atthi nukho āvuso ettha kocipi viraddhadoso.

Question – Friend, is there anyone here who has committed an offense?

Vissajjanā – natthi bhante.

Reply – Venerable sir, there is none.

Bhikkhunī vibhaṅgapācittiya

The Bhikkhunī Analysis, Pācittiya

1. Lasuṇavagga, 1. paṭhama sikkhāpucchā

1. The Garlic Chapter, 1. The First Question on the Training Rule

Pucchā – yaṃ [M 1.0207] tena āvuso bhagavatā…pe… sammāsambuddhena lakkhaṇaṃ khādantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question – Friend, where was the Pācittiya offense established by that Blessed One…pe… the Perfectly Self-Enlightened One, for eating garlic? Concerning whom and in what circumstance was it established?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante thullanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, thullanandā bhante bhikkhunī na mattaṃ jānitvā bahuṃ lasuṇaṃ harāpesi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply – At Sāvatthī, venerable sir, it was established concerning the bhikkhunī Thullanandā. Venerable sir, the bhikkhunī Thullanandā, not knowing the measure, had a large quantity of garlic obtained. In that circumstance, venerable sir, it was established.

1. Lasuṇavagga, 6. chaṭṭha sikkhāpucchā

1. The Garlic Chapter, 6. The Sixth Question on the Training Rule

Pucchā – bhikkhussa āvuso bhuñjamānassa annena vā pānena vā vidhūpanena vā upatiṭṭhantiyā bhikkhuniyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question – Friend, where was the Pācittiya offense established for a bhikkhunī who attends upon a bhikkhu who is eating, whether with food or drink or fanning? Concerning whom and in what circumstance was it established?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ [M 1.0208] bhante aññataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, aññatarā bhante bhikkhunī bhikkhussa bhuñjamānassa pānīyena vidhūpanena ca upatiṭṭhati, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply – At Sāvatthī, venerable sir, it was established concerning a certain bhikkhunī. Venerable sir, a certain bhikkhunī attended upon a bhikkhu who was eating, with drinking water and fanning. In that circumstance, venerable sir, it was established.

1. Lasuṇavagga, 8. aṭṭhama sikkhāpucchā

1. The Garlic Chapter, 8. The Eighth Question on the Training Rule

Pucchā – uccāraṃvā āvuso passāvaṃvā saṅkāraṃvā vighāsaṃvā thirokuṭṭe chaḍḍentiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question – Friend, where was the Pācittiya offense established for a bhikkhunī who throws excrement, urine, rubbish, or food scraps over a wall? Concerning whom and in what circumstance was it established?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante aññataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, aññatarā bhante bhikkhunī uccāraṃ tirokuṭṭe chaḍḍesi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply – At Sāvatthī, venerable sir, it was established concerning a certain bhikkhunī. Venerable sir, a certain bhikkhunī threw excrement over a wall. In that circumstance, venerable sir, it was established.

1. Lasuṇavagga, 10. dasama sikkhāpucchā

1. The Garlic Chapter, 10. The Tenth Question on the Training Rule

Pucchā – naccaṃ [M 1.0209] vā āvuso gītaṃ vā vāditaṃ vā dassanāya gacchantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question – Friend, where was the Pācittiya offense established for a bhikkhunī who goes to see dancing, singing, or instrumental music? Concerning whom and in what circumstance was it established?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyo ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhuniyo naccampi gītampi vāditampi dassanāya agamaṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply – At Sāvatthī, venerable sir, it was established concerning the group of six bhikkhunīs. Venerable sir, the group of six bhikkhunīs went to see dancing, singing, and instrumental music. In that circumstance, venerable sir, it was established.

Pucchā – bhikkhupana [M 1.0210] āvuso naccaṃ vā gītaṃ vā vāditaṃ vā dassanāya gacchanto kaṃ nāma āpattiṃ āpajjati.

Question – But, friend, if a bhikkhu goes to see dancing, singing, or instrumental music, what offense does he incur?

Vissajjanā – dukkaṭaṃ bhante āpattiṃ āpajjati.

Reply – Venerable sir, he incurs an offense of wrong-doing.

1. Paṭhama sikkhāpucchā

1. The First Training Question

Pucchā – rattandhakāre āvuso apadīpe purisena saddhiṃ ekenekāya santiṭṭhantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense of pācittiya laid down for a bhikkhunī who stands alone with a man in the darkness of night without a lamp? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what incident was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante aññataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, aññatarā bhante bhikkhunī rattandhakāre apadīpe purisena saddhiṃ ekenekā santiṭṭhi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning a certain bhikkhunī. Venerable sir, a certain bhikkhunī stood alone with a man in the darkness of night without a lamp. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that incident.

2. Andhakāravagga, 2-3. dutiya, tatiya sikkhāpucchā

2. The Darkness Chapter, 2-3. The Second and Third Training Questions

Pucchā – paṭicchanne [M 1.0211] vā āvuso okāse ajjhokāse vā purisena saddhiṃ ekenekāya santiṭṭhantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense of pācittiya laid down for a bhikkhunī who stands alone with a man in a concealed place or in an open space? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what incident was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante aññataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, aññatarā bhante bhikkhunī paṭicchanne okāse ajjhokāse ca purisena saddhiṃ ekenekā santiṭṭhi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning a certain bhikkhunī. Venerable sir, a certain bhikkhunī stood alone with a man in a concealed place and in an open space. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that incident.

2. Andhakāravagga, 8. aṭṭhama sikkhāpucchā

2. The Darkness Chapter, 8. The Eighth Training Question

Pucchā – duggahitena āvuso dūpadhāritena paraṃ ujjhāpentiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense of pācittiya laid down for a bhikkhunī who disparages another with what is poorly grasped and poorly retained? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what incident was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante aññataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, aññatarā bhante bhikkhunī duggahitena dūpadhāritena paraṃ ujjhāpesi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning a certain bhikkhunī. Venerable sir, a certain bhikkhunī disparaged another with what was poorly grasped and poorly retained. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that incident.

2. Andhakāravagga, 9. navama sikkhāpucchā

2. The Darkness Chapter, 9. The Ninth Training Question

Pucchā – attānaṃ [M 1.0212] vā āvuso paraṃ vā nirayena vā brahmacariyena vā abhisapantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense of pācittiya laid down for a bhikkhunī who curses herself or another with hell or with the holy life? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what incident was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante caṇḍakāḷiṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, caṇḍakāḷī bhante bhikkhunī attānampi parampi nirayenapi brahmacariyenapi abhisapi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the bhikkhunī Caṇḍakāḷī. Venerable sir, the bhikkhunī Caṇḍakāḷī cursed herself and others with hell and with the holy life. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that incident.

2. Andhakāravagga, 10. dasama sikkhāpucchā

2. The Darkness Chapter, 10. The Tenth Training Question

Pucchā – attānaṃ [M 1.0213] āvuso vadhitvā vadhitvā rodantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense of pācittiya laid down for a bhikkhunī who cries, having struck and struck herself? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what incident was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante caṇḍakāḷiṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, caṇḍakāḷī bhante bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ bhaṇḍitvā attānaṃ vadhitvā vadhitvā rodi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the bhikkhunī Caṇḍakāḷī. Venerable sir, the bhikkhunī Caṇḍakāḷī, having quarreled with the bhikkhunīs, struck and struck herself and cried. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that incident.

3. Naggavagga, 3. tatiya sikkhāpucchā

3. The Nakedness Chapter, 3. The Third Training Question

Pucchā – bhikkhuniyā [M 1.0214] āvuso cīvaraṃ visibbetvā vā visibbāpetvā vā neva sibbentiyā na sibbāpanāya ussukkaṃ karontiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense of pācittiya laid down for a bhikkhunī who, having unsewn a robe or had it unsewn, neither sews it nor makes an effort to have it sewn? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what incident was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante thullanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, thullanandā bhante bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ visibbāpetvā neva sibbesi na sibbāpanāya ussukkaṃ akāsi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the bhikkhunī Thullanandā. Venerable sir, the bhikkhunī Thullanandā had a bhikkhunī’s robe unsewn, and then neither sewed it nor made an effort to have it sewn. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that incident.

3. Naggavagga, 8. aṭṭhama sikkhāpucchā

3. The Nakedness Chapter, 8. The Eighth Training Question

Pucchā – agārikassa [M 1.0215] vā āvuso paribbājakassa vā paribbājikāya vā samaṇacīvaraṃ dentiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense of pācittiya laid down for a bhikkhunī giving a monastic robe to a householder, a male wanderer, or a female wanderer? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what incident was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante thullanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, thullanandā bhante bhikkhunī agārikassa samaṇacīvaraṃ adāsi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the bhikkhunī Thullanandā. Venerable sir, the bhikkhunī Thullanandā gave a monastic robe to a householder. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that incident.

4. Tuvaṭṭavagga, 1-2. paṭhama, dutiya sikkhāpucchā

4. The Chapter on Lying Together, 1-2. The First and Second Training Questions

Pucchā – dvinnaṃ āvuso bhikkhunīnaṃ ekamañce vā ekattharaṇapāvuraṇe vā tuvaṭṭentīnaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense of pācittiya laid down for two bhikkhunīs who lie together on the same bed or under the same covering? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what incident was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante sambahulā bhikkhuniyo ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhuniyo dve ekamañce tuvaṭṭesuṃ, ekattharaṇapāvuraṇe tuvaṭṭesuṃ, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning several bhikkhunīs. Venerable sir, several bhikkhunīs lay together, two to a bed, and they lay under one covering. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that incident.

4. Tuvaṭṭavagga, 3. tatiya sikkhāpucchā

4. The Chapter on Lying Together, 3. The Third Training Question

Pucchā – bhikkhuniyā [M 1.0216] āvuso sañcicca aphāsuṃ karontiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense of pācittiya laid down for a bhikkhunī who intentionally causes discomfort? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what incident was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante thullanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, thullanandā bhante bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā sañcicca aphāsuṃ akāsi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the bhikkhunī Thullanandā. Venerable sir, the bhikkhunī Thullanandā intentionally caused discomfort to a bhikkhunī. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that incident.

4. Tuvaṭṭavagga, 4. catuttha sikkhāpucchā

4. The Chapter on Lying Together, 4. The Fourth Training Question

Pucchā – dukkhitaṃ [M 1.0217] āvuso sahajīviniṃ neva upaṭṭhentiyā na upaṭṭhāpanāya ussukkaṃ karontiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: Friend, where was the offense of pācittiya laid down for a bhikkhunī who neither attends to her sick co-resident nor makes an effort to have her attended to? Concerning whom was it laid down, and in what incident was it laid down?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante thullanandaṃyeva bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, thullanandā bhante bhikkhunī dukkhitaṃ sahajīviniṃ nevaupaṭṭhesi na upaṭṭhāpanāya ussukkaṃ akāsi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the bhikkhunī Thullanandā. Venerable sir, the bhikkhunī Thullanandā neither attended to her sick co-resident nor made an effort to have her attended to. Venerable sir, it was laid down concerning that incident.

4. Tuvaṭṭavagga, 5. pañcama sikkhāpucchā

4. The Chapter on Lying Together, The Fifth Training Question

Pucchā – bhikkhuniyā [M 1.0218] āvuso upassayaṃ datvā kupitāya anattamanāya nikkaḍḍhantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the pācittiya rule laid down for a bhikkhunī who, having given lodging, angrily and displeasedly drives out another bhikkhunī? Concerning whom and on what occasion was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante thullanandaṃyeva bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, thullanandā bhante bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā upassayaṃ datvā kupitā anattamanā nikkaḍḍhi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the bhikkhunī Thullanandā. Venerable sir, the bhikkhunī Thullanandā, having given lodging to a bhikkhunī, became angry and displeased, and drove her out. Venerable sir, it was laid down on that occasion.”

4. Tuvaṭṭavagga, 6. chaṭṭha sikkhāpucchā

4. The Chapter on Lying Together, The Sixth Training Question

Pucchā – saṃsaṭṭhāya [M 1.0219] āvuso bhikkhuniyā yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissajjantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the pācittiya rule laid down for a bhikkhunī who, when admonished up to the third time about associating closely, does not relinquish it? Concerning whom and on what occasion was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante caṇḍakāḷiṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, caṇḍakāḷī bhante bhikkhunī saṃsaṭṭhā vihari, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the bhikkhunī Caṇḍakāḷī. Venerable sir, the bhikkhunī Caṇḍakāḷī lived in close association. Venerable sir, it was laid down on that occasion.”

4. Tuvaṭṭavagga, 7-8. sattama aṭṭhama sikkhāpucchā

4. The Chapter on Lying Together, The Seventh and Eighth Training Questions

Pucchā – antoraṭṭhe [M 1.0220] vā āvuso tiroraṭṭhe vā sāsaṅkasammate sappaṭibhaye asatthikāya cārikaṃ carantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the pācittiya rule laid down for a bhikkhunī who wanders without an escort in a region regarded as dangerous and alarming, whether within the country or in outlying districts? Concerning whom and on what occasion was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante sambahulā bhikkhuniyo ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhuniyo antoraṭṭhepi tiroraṭṭhepi sāsaṅkasammate sappaṭibhaye asatthikāyo cārikaṃ cariṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning several bhikkhunīs. Venerable sir, several bhikkhunīs traveled without an escort in regions regarded as dangerous and alarming, both within the country and in outlying districts. Venerable sir, it was laid down on that occasion.”

4. Tuvaṭṭavagga, 9. navama sikkhāpucchā

4. The Chapter on Lying Together, The Ninth Training Question

Pucchā – antovassaṃ āvuso cārikaṃ carantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the pācittiya rule laid down for a bhikkhunī who travels during the rains? Concerning whom and on what occasion was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – rājagahe bhante sambahulā bhikkhuniyo ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhuniyo antovassaṃ cārikaṃ pakkamiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down at Rājagaha concerning several bhikkhunīs. Venerable sir, several bhikkhunīs departed on tour during the rains. Venerable sir, it was laid down on that occasion.”

4. Tuvaṭṭavagga, 10. dasama sikkhāpucchā

4. The Chapter on Lying Together, The Tenth Training Question

Pucchā – vassaṃ [M 1.0221] vuṭṭhāya āvuso bhikkhuniyā cārikaṃ na pakkamantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the pācittiya rule laid down for a bhikkhunī who does not set out on tour after the rains residence? Concerning whom and on what occasion was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – rājagahe bhante sambahulā bhikkhuniyo ārabbhaṃ paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhuniyo vassaṃvuṭṭhā cārikaṃ na pakkamiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down at Rājagaha concerning several bhikkhunīs. Venerable sir, several bhikkhunīs, having completed the rains residence, did not depart on tour. Venerable sir, it was laid down on that occasion.”

Pucchā – kassa [M 1.0222] āvuso vacanaṃ.

Question: “Friend, whose utterance is this?”

Vissajjanā – bhagavato bhante vacanaṃ arahato sammāsambuddhassa.

Reply: “Venerable sir, this is the utterance of the Blessed One, the Arahant, the Perfectly Enlightened One.”

Pucchā – kenāvuso ābhataṃ.

Question: “Friend, by whom was it brought?”

Vissajjanā – paramparāya bhante vacanaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it is an utterance handed down by tradition.”

Suṇātu me bhante saṅgho…pe… saṃgāyeyyāma.

“Venerable sir, may the Sangha hear me… let us recite.”

5. Cittāgāravagga, 1. paṭhama sikkhāpucchā

5. The Chapter on Picture Galleries, The First Training Question

Pucchā – yaṃ tena āvuso bhagavatā…pe… sammāsambuddhena rājāgāraṃ vā cittāgāraṃ vā ārāmaṃ vā uyyānaṃ vā pokkharaṇiṃ vā dassanāya gacchantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the pācittiya rule laid down by that Blessed One… the Perfectly Enlightened One, for a bhikkhunī who goes to see a royal palace, a picture gallery, a monastery, a park, or a lotus pond? Concerning whom and on what occasion was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ [M 1.0223] bhante chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyo ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhuniyo rājāgārampi cittāgārampi dassanāya gacchiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the group-of-six bhikkhunīs. Venerable sir, the group-of-six bhikkhunīs went to see a royal palace and a picture gallery. Venerable sir, it was laid down on that occasion.”

5. Cittāgāravagga, 4. catuttha sikkhāpucchā

5. The Chapter on Picture Galleries, The Fourth Training Question

Pucchā – gihiveyyāvaccaṃ āvuso karontiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the pācittiya rule laid down for a bhikkhunī who performs services for a layperson? Concerning whom and on what occasion was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante sambahulā bhikkhuniyo ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhuniyogihi veyyāvaccaṃ akaṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning several bhikkhunīs. Venerable sir, several bhikkhunīs performed services for laypeople. Venerable sir, it was laid down on that occasion.”

5. Cittāgāravagga, 6. chaṭṭha sikkhāpucchā

5. The Chapter on Picture Galleries, The Sixth Training Question

Pucchā – agārikassa [M 1.0224] vā āvuso paribbājakassa vā paribbājikāya vā sahatthā khādanīyaṃ bhojanīyaṃ dentiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question: “Friend, where was the pācittiya rule laid down for a bhikkhunī who gives staple or non-staple food with her own hand to a householder, a male wanderer, or a female wanderer? Concerning whom and on what occasion was it laid down?”

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante thullanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha paññattaṃ, thullanandā bhante bhikkhunī agārikassa sahatthā khādanīyampi bhojanīyampi adāsi, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply: “Venerable sir, it was laid down at Sāvatthī concerning the bhikkhunī Thullanandā. Venerable sir, the bhikkhunī Thullanandā gave staple and non-staple food with her own hand to a householder. Venerable sir, it was laid down on that occasion.”

5. Cittāgāravagga, 9-10. navama, dasama sikkhāpucchā

5. The Chapter on Picture Galleries, The Ninth and Tenth Training Questions

Pucchā – tiracchānavijjaṃ āvuso pariyāpuṇantiyā ca vācentiyā ca pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question – Friend, where was the training rule entailing expiation established for a bhikkhunī who learns or teaches animal lore? Concerning whom and in what matter was it established?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyo ārabbha paññattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhante bhikkhuniyo tiracchānavijjaṃ pariyāpuṇiṃsuceva vācesuñca, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Reply – Venerable sir, it was established at Sāvatthī concerning the group of six bhikkhunīs. Venerable sir, the group of six bhikkhunīs learned and taught animal lore. It was established, venerable sir, concerning that matter.

Pucchā – bhikkhu [M 1.0225] pana āvuso tādisaṃ tiracchānavijjaṃ pariyāpuṇanto ca vācento ca kiṃ nāma āpattiṃ āpajjati.

Question – But, friend, what offense does a bhikkhu incur by learning and teaching such animal lore?

Vissajjanā – dukkaṭaṃ bhante āpattiṃ āpajjati.

Reply – Venerable sir, he incurs an offense of wrong-doing.

6. Ārāmavagga, 1. paṭhama sikkhāpucchā

6. The Chapter on Monasteries, 1. The First Training Question.

Pucchā – jānaṃ āvuso sabhikkhukaṃ ārāmaṃ anāpucchā pavisantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha paññattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.

Question – Friend, where was the training rule entailing expiation established for a bhikkhunī who, knowing, enters a monastery where bhikkhus are residing without asking permission? Concerning whom and in what matter was it established?

Vissajjanā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhante sambahulā bhikkhuniyo ārabbha paññattaṃ, sambahulā bhante bhikkhuniyo ārāmaṃ anāpucchā pavisiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhante vatthusmiṃ paññattaṃ.
